# CLU Future-Work & Untested-Extensions Register

> **Purpose.** The living catalogue of **what the currently-scoped shorts do NOT show** — scientific extensions, unprobed regimes, and scope boundaries we have *clarified* but not *demonstrated*. This is the honest map of the program's edge, so we never lose sight of an unshown claim when a reviewer (or we) ask "does this generalize?"
>
> **Distinct from siblings:** `negative_results.md` = things we TRIED and they failed/nulled · `research_roadmap.md` §backlog = tasks scheduled-or-deferred (execution planning) · **this file** = *scientific* extensions not yet attempted (the "known unknowns" that scope every paper's claims and feed its **Future Work** section + the ICLR long).
>
> **⟲ Living-document protocol (2026-07-08).** Updated **every wave** by `doc-curator` (and by the Hub when a brainstorm/analysis surfaces a new boundary). Entries are never deleted — when one is demonstrated, mark it `→ SHOWN (wave-N, report)` and keep it as the provenance trail. Each entry: **what we haven't shown · why it matters · where it lands (paper future-work / task / vertical) · status**.
> Status tags: `OPEN` (untouched) · `THEORY-ONLY` (derived, not measured) · `TASKED(<slug>)` · `PARKED(reason)` · `→ SHOWN`.

## V2 — Memory as broken symmetry (single-register, SO(2))

- **Non-abelian / multi-Goldstone registers.** Everything shown is **SO(2) (abelian, one Goldstone)**. The single-mode latch + GMOR are group-agnostic *by derivation* ⇒ dim(G/H) parallel registers for any compact G — but this is `THEORY-ONLY`. Three unshown pieces: (i) **multiplicity** dim(G/H) as a channel bank; (ii) **kinetic-isotropy/Schur** — non-abelian charge conservation needs *equal inertial masses within a multiplet* (trivial for SO(2), a real design constraint for SO(3)/SU(N)); (iii) **non-abelian Goldstone self-interaction** — curved coset (nonlinear σ-model) means the registers may *couple*, breaking the clean independent-register picture. → **V4** / V2 future-work. Status: `THEORY-ONLY` (i,ii), `OPEN` (iii, genuinely unexplored physics).
- **Task-symmetry mismatch.** Shown only for the **matched** case (SO(2) model symmetry on SO(2) circle data). Untested: model symmetry *larger* than task (imposed SO(2) on arc/asymmetric data → over-invariance, the model is blind to angular task structure) and the graceful-degradation boundary between "useful register" and "harmful over-generalization." → V2 future-work. Status: `OPEN`.
- **Learned (vs analytic) explicit breaking.** The pseudo-Goldstone GMOR law (n₁/₂∝1/δ) was demonstrated by **adding an analytic tilt δ** (TiltedPotential). Untested: does a CLU trained on *approximately-symmetric data* learn the breaking such that its retention lifetime tracks the data's degree of asymmetry? (This would be the strongest "task-symmetry sets memory lifetime" claim, from data rather than by hand.) Status: `OPEN`.
- **Emergent SSB, characterized.** The free-MLP arm self-breaks *weakly* (δ≈0.01–0.06, 13–14 orders worse than designed). Untested: can emergent SSB be *induced* stronger (curriculum, symmetry-encouraging reg, longer training) without designing it in — and where is the designed/emergent Pareto? Status: `OPEN` (ties to the Hyp-3 mass doctrine).
  - > **Update (wave-13, `v5-gate` R1 → N46): the question is now much sharper, and half-answered in the negative.** The emergent coset is **not a near-Goldstone at all** — it is a **mid-spectrum massive mode**, only **1.7–4.9× softer than the *stiffest* direction in the whole spectrum** (`1−|λ_coset| = 1.06e-3 … 2.96e-3` vs designed `≤1.1e-15`), and **any written coset value relaxes completely** (`|retained| ≤ 2.1e-3`). Its capacity is `log₂(2–3 washboard minima) ≈ **1–1.6 bits**`, not a continuum. **⇒ "CHLU's flat directions" do not emerge; they must be designed in.** The Pareto question survives, but it is now *"can induction close a 12-order gap?"* — and the honest prior is no. **Still genuinely OPEN:** whether *any* induction protocol (curriculum, symmetry reg, longer training) moves `1−|λ_coset|` by even one order. **Cheap and unattempted.** Status: `OPEN` (reframed by N46).
  - > **Companion boundary (wave-13):** the emergent washboard is a **naturally occurring `tilt_delta`** — a free pseudo-Goldstone testbed for the Arrhenius hopping law `n_hop ∝ e^{2δr*/T}`, requiring **no retraining** (`v5-gate` R5). Status: `OPEN` (small).
- **Information PROPAGATION (the Welling axis).** V2 is retention/memory (value persists in *time* at one unit). Untested and orthogonal: using Goldstone modes to **propagate signal across depth/units without attenuation** (Welling's regime). The natural bridge from single-register memory to a transport channel. → V4 / ICLR. Status: `OPEN`.
- **Equivariant-control input wrapper.** CLU has no native velocity/input ingestion, so it cannot currently enter Mo's *supervised* path-integration RMSE axis — the head-to-head is retention-only. A momentum-kick-along-coset-tangent wrapper would complete it in both directions. Status: `OPEN` (flagged since v2-prefreeze-baselines).
- **Width-matched per-step cost (new — wave-10, `v2-referee-2` missing-experiment #4).** *What we haven't shown:* the per-step cost of a **width-matched** CLU (h16 potential) against the h16 LSTM/LEM baselines. *Why it matters:* the honest per-step factors that retire the "≈4×" (6.2× LSTM / 3.1× LEM wall; 14–15× FLOPs; retention-per-compute inverts 23.5×/14.6× — N40/CM-4) are measured at **CLU h64 vs baselines h16**, so they conflate the *two ∇V backprops* (intrinsic, sign-robust) with the *width* (incidental). A width-matched number would let §3.3 state a **width-controlled** per-step factor instead of a confounded one. *Where it lands:* V2 §3.3 / App H fortification; not blocking (the inversion's **sign** is robust — the Verlet step needs two potential-gradient evals). Status: `OPEN` (fortification candidate; flagged in `v2-referee-experiments` follow-ups).

## V1 — Paid access / certified test-time compute

- **Learned entrance-steering (the crux).** Paid-access reach was shown at **oracle channel placement**. The true engineering problem — *learning* where to place wormhole entrances so trajectories arrive at them under the Verlet flow — is untested and is the likely failure point at scale. Explicitly the ICLR-long target per the Head. Status: `OPEN` (theory: paid-access-theory open-risk 1).
- **MH-kernel experiment (Thread 9).** Squeeze-MH is a certified kernel *in theory* (CM-14, toy EBMs). Untested on trained CLU checkpoints: the w9 experiment spec (vs learned-τ AND router baselines; measure the latch-diffusion erosion rate live). Kill criterion in the spec. Status: `THEORY-ONLY` → w9-spec in `thread9-mh-kernel.md`.
- **Noise-robustness of the gate (the noise wall).** The dominant V1 negative: the relaxation gate loses to Hopfield's single matvec under cue noise (0/6 close). Untested *cures*: noise-aware τ, longer relax budget, denoising init. The paper states it as a boundary (future work), not solved. Status: `OPEN` (the V1-narrative threat to settle before ICLR).
- **γ>0 governor re-absorption certificate.** Certificates verified at γ=0 (sharp box). The `t_reabsorb≈2ζ/γ_c` timing row is only leading-order at γ=0. Status: `OPEN`.
- **Harder-band router regime.** The router beat energy-gating on a linearly-separable-cue task. Untested: any regime (overlapping clusters / higher kv / smaller embed) where the energy signal *ever* adds routing value — or an honest "it never does at any scale." Status: `OPEN`.
- **Intra-unit wormhole: learned placement + non-coercive exits.** Built + certified at oracle placement, coercive wells. Untested: learned placement, and BIBO when a jump exits into a non-coercive Deep/Conv region. Status: `OPEN`.
- **The certificate's PAYOFF, measured (new — wave-10, `v1-referee` F3).** *What we haven't shown:* a single arm in which the certificate-free baseline **demonstrably violates a guarantee the wormhole preserves** — router jump → non-coercive exit → BIBO blow-up, or router erases the latch where the wormhole transports it by exact $p^\top X\Delta$. *Why it matters:* today "the wormhole **beats** a no-physics router" is **definitional, not demonstrated** — the wormhole's det J=1 label has no shown downstream consequence anywhere in V1, while the same 449-param router beats the energy-gated edge in **both FLOPs and accuracy** (router 1.000/0.948 @8.81e7 vs gated 0.887/0.715 @1.18e8; CM-7/N24). The referee calls this "the sharpest quotable inversion" and **the one addition that lifts V1 above weak-accept** — the only place the physics could show a *measured* learned-system win. *Where it lands:* V1 §3.2 (or an honest softening to "reaches with a receipt the router cannot supply"); ICLR long. Status: `TASKED(v1-certificate-payoff, w11)` — check `paid-access-experiments` logs first; if a router-latch/router-exit failure is already logged this is a **wiring note, not an experiment**.

## V3 — The CLU lattice

- **Reversible-BPTT in the real trainer + accelerator.** Shown: gradient mechanics on *untrained* models, final-state loss, CPU (946× memory, ≈0.9× wall). Untested: wiring into `train_chlu` (per-step-loss reverse scan); GPU/HBM peak-memory and honest wall-time at T≳4k where standard BPTT OOMs; the checkpointing-O(√T) Pareto baseline; mixed-γ segment accounting (how much of a real network stays O(1)-trainable). This is the headline ICLR *systems* result, currently only structurally demonstrated. Status: `OPEN`.
- **Interference — the unmeasured channels.** Firewall shown at the θ-kernel level (∝κ², O(1)-vs-O(N), N≤8). Untested: (i) trajectory-mediated banding channel (banding changes *which states* training visits); (ii) irrep/symmetry firewall (F5 cat ii); (iii) block-structured-monolith control (is it modularity or separate nets?); (iv) dynamical interference *half-life* (how long an interference event persists); (v) interference at N≫8. Status: `OPEN` (v3-interference-ntk follow-ups).
  - > **Wave-10 update (doc-curator; source `v3-referee` MF-1 / SF-1 / N-3 / missing-experiment list).** Four of these five are now **referee-named** — they moved from our own follow-up list to a reviewer's stated objections, which raises their priority and fixes where they land. Per-item status:
    > - **(iii) block-structured monolith** — the referee's *"is it modularity or is it just separate nets?"*, named as **a reviewer's first question** and as hostile-quote #2. Discriminating control: a single V with explicit block-diagonal structure. Status: `TASKED(v3-interference-extra, w11)`.
    > - **(v)→ intermediate N.** The headline is measured at exactly **N∈{4,8}**; across that single doubling modular S grew **6.79×10⁻⁵ → 1.74×10⁻⁴ (×2.56)** and monolith **0.635 → 1.384 (×2.18)** — *the same empirical slope* (`v3-referee` MF-1, from `v3-interference-ntk/interference_init.json` + `v3-scaling-figure`). Adding **N∈{2,6}** is what would convert a structural claim into a defensible empirical growth-rate claim. Status: `TASKED(v3-interference-extra, w11)`; N≫8 remains `OPEN`.
    > - **(i) trajectory-mediated channel** — §3.2 measures the *potential NTK / force field*; banding changes which loci drive updates. Referee: "repeat §3.2 with loci driven from actual banded-vs-uniform rollouts." Status: `OPEN` (referee-named).
    > - **(iv) dynamical interference half-life** — load-bearing for licensing the *"monolith interferes with itself more than it stores" (S=1.38>1)* gloss as a **storage** rather than force-field statement (`v3-referee` SF-1: S=1 is a normalization reference line, not a measured capacity threshold). Status: `OPEN` (referee-named; gates a wording, not a result).
    > - **(ii) irrep firewall** — unchanged `OPEN`.
- **Band-selection on non-separable timescales.** FFT selector = oracle *only when timescales are spectrally separable*. Untested: overlapping/noisy timescales, where the selector-oracle gap opens. Status: `OPEN`.
- **Capacity/scale.** kv≤128, dim 32, N≤8 throughout. Untested: kv160/192 + the true capacity wall vs epoch-budget wall separation; CLU-dim sweep at fixed kv; the lattice beyond N=8. Status: `OPEN`.

## V4 — Symmetry-allocated memory bank (candidate vertical; Head-named 2026-07-08)

- The multi-Goldstone / non-abelian / **dim(G/H)-as-channel-count** work: turns V2's *one register* into a *memory bank* and is the honest home for the Welling-style **propagation** flavor + the **EFT-of-memory** (HEFT/SMEFT coset parameterizations) framing. Absorbs the V2 non-abelian items above. Go/no-go **mid-Aug**, colleague-dependent (Manchester HEFT). Status: `PARKED(mid-Aug go/no-go)` — Hub to write V4 scoping when the Head calls it.
- **Deep-dive V4 seeds (proven+verified toy; `v2-symmetry-deepdive` §7):** (a) **abelian (torus) cosets = exactly independent registers** — the design rule; on a symmetric coset the stored VALUE is order-insensitive to O(α⁵) under the CLU's native write while the addressing BASIS rotates by holonomy ∝α² ⇒ non-abelian ≠ messy (refutes the naive [X_a,X_b]≠0 intuition). (b) Cross-talk is **multiplicative (gain/addressing) and idle-free** (F_φ²(θ)=Mf²sin²θ). (c) **Custodial retention anisotropy (F-4):** within a multiplet, retention ratio = inertial-mass ratio n₁/n₂=m₁/m₂, δ-independent. (d) Gell-Mann–Okubo → **sum rules among inverse retention half-lives**. Status: `THEORY-ONLY` (toy-verified) → V4.
- **Relativistic "running decay constant" (O5).** ~~`OPEN — active in v2-symmetry-deepdive`~~ **→ SHOWN (wave-13, `v2-symmetry-deepdive` v1.1 §7bis R0–R2).** `F_Q²(p) = F_Q²(0)·cosh ζ = M_eff r*²·γ_L(p)` — **the decay constant is Lorentz-enhanced by the write current**; the write *current* `Q = r*p` is unbounded while the write *speed* `θ̇_max = c/(√M_ch r*)` is capped, and `F_Q` is exactly the ratio. Verified on a stiff ring: `F_Q²_meas/(M_eff r̄² cosh ζ)` = 0.99999 → 0.9982 for `p₀ = 0.2 → 10`. **Honest flag (carry it):** this is **NOT a ChPT form factor** — Lorentz covariance fixes `⟨0|A_μ|π(p)⟩ = i f_π p_μ` to be exactly linear in `p`, so *there is no running*. Ours is the relativistic **rotor**: the coset angle's moment of inertia is `γ_L`-enhanced. **Use "running decay constant" as a CLU term, never as a QCD claim.** GMOR, `ρ_CLU` and the kinetic-spurion blindness theorem all **survive at rest** with `M_eff = m₀M` (`μ²F_Q²(0) = δΣ` to 0.0 / 3.5e-18 across four `(m₀,M)` pairs).

## Relativistic register physics (deep-dive §7bis, wave-13 — O5 closed, four new boundaries + one new defect)

> *These are Nature-MI + ICLR-long material. Per the G7 LONGS mandate they do **not** widen any short.* The single short-side change is a **correction**, not an addition: V2 App-F's `σ*` statement needs a kinetic-mode qualifier (→ `f5-corrigendum-2`).

- **⭐ R5 — the causal retention–bandwidth bound (THEORY-ONLY, now under test).** Overdamped retention is `n₁/₂ ≈ 2γln2/((2−γ)(εμ)²)` and GMOR gives `μ² = δΣ/F²`, so `n₁/₂ ∝ F²`; but bandwidth `θ̇_max = c√m₀/F ∝ 1/F`. **The decay constant cancels from the product:**
  ```
  n₁/₂ · θ̇_max²  =  2γ ln2 · m₀c² / ( (2−γ) ε² · δΣ )        — independent of M and r*
  ```
  *Verified* on the actual damped relativistic map (`δ=0.02, γ=ε=0.05`, `M ∈ {0.5,1,2,4}`): products 681.7 / 696.0 / 702.9 / 706.4 vs predicted **709.7** (ratios 0.9605 → 0.9952); the residual is **exactly** the deep-overdamped correction `(εμ/γ)²` (deficits 3.95/1.94/0.96/0.48% vs `(εμ/γ)²` = 4.00/2.00/1.00/0.50%). Thermal analogue: `n_T·θ̇_max² = Δ²γ·m₀c²/(ε²T(2−γ))` — same structure, `T` replacing `δΣ` as the forgetting drive. **Reading:** `F` buys robustness (`n₁/₂ ∝ F²`) and pays in write bandwidth. **Only raising `c` (or `m₀`), or lowering the breaking, buys both.** ⚠ **In Newtonian mode `θ̇` is unbounded ⇒ no bandwidth constraint and no bound: this trade-off is *created by the causal governor*.** *Why it matters:* it turns "M is the budget allocator" from a slogan into a **candidate conservation law.** **→ SHOWN on TRAINED checkpoints (wave-14, `rb-bound-trained`, F-12):** across a 16× inertial-mass band `n₁/₂` moves **16.6×** (342.4→5690.3) and `θ̇_max` moves **4.0×** (1.4102→0.3526) in opposite directions, while the **product moves 1.0386× vs pre-registered 1.0388×, 5/5 seeds**, every band's `P/P₀` matching prereg to **≤4e-11**. *If `M` bought both, `P` would span 16×; it spans 3.9%.* ⚠ **Two scope clauses R5 did not state and any paper use MUST carry: (1) it is a SATURATED-WRITE law** — below the causal cap the relativistic arm reproduces Newtonian `P ∝ 1/M` (at fixed finite impulse the Newtonian product spreads **15.40×**; the relativistic spread collapses `15.40× → 1.0386×` **as the write saturates the cap** — *the governor CREATES the conservation law*). **(2) The residual coefficient is `((2−γ)/2)²(εμ/γ)²`, not bare `(εμ/γ)²`** (the deep-dive quoted the γ→0 limit; N55). The Newtonian null is sharper than "undefined": `θ̇(p₀)=p₀/(M_ch r*)` exactly (10 digits, `p₀≤1e4`) ⇒ `sup_p P = ∞`. Status: **`→ SHOWN (wave-14, rb-bound-trained)` — saturated-write, relativistic-mode only; the Newtonian null is half the result.**
- **R3 — the relativistic latch stores *rapidity*, not momentum (an automatic companding encoder).** Along an exactly flat direction, `pₙ = (1−γ)ⁿp₀` exactly and `Δθ = ε·θ̇_max·Σ tanh ζₙ`, `sinh ζₙ = (1−γ)ⁿ sinh ζ₀` (**exact**; verified against the simulated map to `2.2e-15`). Newtonian limit recovers F5's latch law `Δθ = εp₀/(M_eff r*γ)`; ultra-relativistic limit gives `Δθ → θ̇_max·ζ₀/γ_c` — **linear in rapidity, logarithmic in the impulse.** Concretely (`M=0.8, ε=γ=0.05`): a write impulse `p₀ = 500` stores `Δθ = 7.678` where the Newtonian latch would store **625.0**. *Scope:* exact for a flat direction with stiff transverse confinement; on a **curved** coset a hard write leaves the vacuum manifold (centrifugal excursion `∝ (θ̇r*)²`). ⚠ **Corrected (wave-14, `causal-memory-floor`, N54): the transport law is a STIFF-RING idealization that fails at the 1% level by `ζ₀ ≈ 0.19` on the *actual trained* ring — NOT the "0.2% at ζ≤3" this entry claimed.** The finite radial stiffness lets a large-rapidity write dislodge the state off the orbit (the natural failure mode of a companding register — O7). The exact law survives as the stiff-confinement limit (2.2e-15 there).   - *"Raise γ to protect the registers"* — **exactly right, and free.** Acceptance is γ-independent by construction; γ only lengthens the register's life, linearly. *Verified:* `N_moves` = 16.84 → 20.32 → 30.74 → 53.13 over γ ∈ {0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20}; the `N_moves` ratio equals the FPT ratio **to 4 decimals**, confirming the exact factorisation `N_moves = p_accept × E[N_FPT]`.
  - *"Anneal T to search structure"* — **needs a caveat.** `N_pred ∝ T⁻¹e^{−B/T}` has an **interior optimum at `T* = B`**, not at `T→0` and not at `T→∞`. **Cold is not free:** at `T ≪ B` the search freezes exponentially while erasure only slows as `1/T`. *Verified in the deep-diffusive regime:* predicted argmax exactly at `T = B = 0.05`, measured argmax one grid step away (0.06), curve flat to 1.3% across [0.05, 0.06].
  - Honest cost of γ: it does not slow structural search *per step*, but it slows the **massive** sector's relaxation (`rate ∝ ε²μ²/γ`, overdamped) — so if `ΔH` depends on equilibrated massive modes, **the ledger goes stale.**
  - Also *inverts* Thread-9's "certified retry erodes the latch" into a fix (run MH segments at high γ). Status: `→ SHOWN (wave-13)`, high design value. **New falsifiable for V5.**
