**New w26 fold (twelve entries, schema-complete):**

- **⭐⭐ Does the unclamping survive d = 6 and d = 8? — the single measurement that decides whether R2 closes as a law or an artefact.** *What we haven't shown:* everything in N109 is **d = 4**. The multi-channel code may close the learned/designed gap at every d — in which case the R2 figure returns as **`K_learned(d) = K_designed(d) = 4·2^d` with a mechanism attached** — or only at d = 4, which is a different and still publishable claim. *Why it matters:* it is the difference between *"we moved a wall"* and *"we changed a law"*, and **Candidate 3 (capacity-per-byte) rests on it.** *Cheapest decisive cell (the engineer's own):* **d = 6, K = 64 and K = 128, m = 4, 3 seeds**, plus the designed arm at the same cells. Status: **`TASKED (r2-d-sweep-close)` (C1W27)** — the wave headline. ⛔ **A FAIL is a clean close and is to be reported as the result, not as a gap.**
- **⭐ The arm(a) × arm(b) combination — untested, and they should compose.** *What we haven't shown:* arm (b) is **read-side** and arm (a) is **code-side**, so nothing forbids stacking them; the driver already supports it and it was **NOT RUN** (compute). *Why it matters:* if they compose, the d = 4 result is a *ladder*, not a single rung; if they do not, that is a mechanism statement about the address phase. Status: **`TASKED (r2-d-sweep-close)` (C1W27)** — with a registered sign prediction.
- **⭐ Capacity-per-byte / bits-per-parameter, re-derived — the whole basis of Candidate 3, and nobody has done it.** *What we haven't shown:* w21's **~1.3-vs-2 bits-per-param** was measured **under** the reach ceiling and is **STALE**. w26 shows only *items-per-parameter at one cell* rising **×2.00** (annealed read, zero extra params) to **×5.60** — the numerator moved, the ratio was never re-computed. *Why it matters:* the Hub's own strategic read is that **Candidate 3 is now the strongest of the three primary-claim routes precisely because the ceiling moved** — and it is unevidenced until this is measured. Status: **`TASKED (r2-d-sweep-close)` (C1W27), conditional** — *only if* the wall moves at d ≥ 6.
- **⭐ The P2 waitlist — the difference between "exact below capacity" and "exact, period."** *What we haven't shown:* counterfactual exactness over the **offered** stream. At overflow the shipped canonical build is **not** exact (`AUC(n_live)` = 1.000, `AUC(s4)` = 0.914, byte-equal 0.000) because a refused background item does not counterfactually return. *Why it matters:* **the overflow regime is the realistic one for a bounded store**, and it is the single qualifier on every R1 sentence. *Spec:* ~20 lines (keep refused records in a side dict; re-run admission by priority on any delete). ⭐ **The acceptance test is a number *and a curve*: overflow `AUC(n_live)` 1.000 → 0.500 and byte-equal 0.000 → 1.000, reported across the 2/4/6/8-offer load curve.** Status: **`TASKED (deletion-waitlist-stiffness)` (C1W27).** ⛔ **Its outcome must not be pre-written anywhere.**
- **⭐ Option (d) gated stiffness — and `R₅₀` re-measured under it (the differentiator has never been checked under the fix).** *What we haven't shown:* whether the **retrieval-geometry** differentiator N108 leads with (`R₅₀` contracting **1.146 → 0.752** vs a TTL's constant ≈0.77) survives when the payload channel is gated. The theorist's toy measured **retention 1.000, payload-independent, to the floor** at `g₀ = amp_floor` — but **the on-site curve becomes a step (the TTL shape)**, and `R₅₀` was **not measured**. *Why it matters:* if `R₅₀` flattens too, the lifetimes dial loses its only caveat-free claim; if it survives, the dial is repaired without cost. *Also owed in the same run:* the **graded** configuration (`g₀ ≪ amp_floor` + 4× read) as the **compute-adaptive-read** demonstration. ⚠ **The normaliser must not be floored** (that version refuted the theorist's own P2.7). Status: **`TASKED (deletion-waitlist-stiffness)` (C1W27)** — behind a flag, **defaults OFF**.
- **⭐⭐ Is there ANY read for which the settle beats arg-min over the same wells? — the program's central open question, now stated as one.** *What we haven't shown:* a single regime in which the damped-Verlet settle beats the same store with the settle deleted. Six byte budgets × 3 seeds say **no** on clean-query classification (≈1 pp worse on both axes, N114); the one non-losing datum is the **corrupted-query, no-constructible-oracle** cell, where it **ties** (N103). *Why it matters:* it is the genuine-win bar applied to the primitive itself, and **it is answerable cheaply** — the same-keys launder is a one-line change to any harness. *Concrete leads:* corrupted/ambiguous queries · multi-modal or superposed queries · queries requiring iterative disambiguation · anything where "nearest centre" is genuinely ill-posed. Status: `OPEN` — ⭐ **and it is the highest-value open item in this register.**
- **⛔ The overflow / non-flat-datastore comparison for R1, in EXISTING vocabulary.** *What we haven't shown:* R1 written as **deletion cost at matched utility** using **Ginart Def A.5** and **Sekhari Def 3**, with SISA's shards-vs-accuracy curve as practice precedent and the **flat-datastore row-delete as the mandatory trivial substitute** — ⛔ **without coining a benchmark** and **without any sentence in which the store beats a dict on deletion exactness**. *Why it matters:* Candidate 2 is un-named but **occupied in pieces**; inventing a metric would be scored as reinvention. Status: **`TASKED (r1-positioning-pass)` (C1W27)** — including the **Part-4 replacement string for Head approval** and both scope wordings (pre-/post-waitlist).
- **⭐ An independent causal test of the saddle criterion (★) on a parameter that is not the excursion.** *What we haven't shown:* that `learned_confine` enters the ceiling through `2α|c_i|` **causally** rather than correlationally. *Spec (the theorist's own):* `learned_confine` **0.05 → 0.022** at d=4, K=16, width 0.15, 3 seeds, prediction registered before the run. *Why it matters:* (★) is verified 31/32 **out of sample** but on **one seed per cell**, and this is a ~1 h test on a knob nobody has moved. Status: **`TASKED (learned-confine-causal)` (C1W27).**
- **The second factor of the cell-level capacity law — the neighbour/interference term.** *What we haven't shown:* a model of `P_addr(sep, s, σ_q)`. Criterion (U) is **single-well** and classifies only **8/11** cells on its own, missing exactly the three crowded high-K FAILs (d4K32, d5K64, d6K64), whose reach margins are comfortable ⇒ **those three are a multi-well interference failure nobody has modelled.** *Why it matters:* the complete law is `strict ≈ min(1, a_U/|a|max) × P_addr(·)`, and only the first factor exists. Status: `THEORY-ONLY` — **the theorist's own stated main open question**, handed back explicitly.
- **Address quantization under canonical placement, for φ-derived addresses.** *What we haven't shown:* what it costs to move every write onto a lattice cell (≤ **0.889** away at `d_safe` = 1.54) when the address **carries similarity** rather than being allocator-chosen. Measured harmless where the allocator picks (per-admitted 1.0000); **unknown** for `φ(x)`. *Why it matters:* it is the only thing standing between canonical placement and the CL path — which is why `canonical` + `allow_relocation=False` is currently a **hard error** (impossible by design, not by luck). Status: `OPEN` — **a measurement is required before canonical placement touches φ.**
- **Turn the saturation POINT into a saturation LAW, and settle where the launder gap goes.** *What we haven't shown:* the store's fill curve above 314 000 floats. Saturation was measured at exactly **one** budget (6 085 ± 66 of 7 658 wells, admitted 0.57–0.62, `refused_full = 0`). *Why it matters:* the source's own prediction is that **CLU flattens at ~6 100–6 500 wells while the ring buffer keeps improving to 18 000+ keys, so the launder gap should RE-OPEN above P6 — and if it does not, the store's geometry is not the limiter and something else is.** That is a falsifiable statement about the packing law inside a benchmark. *Cost:* ~20 min (2×) and ~40 min (4×) per seed. Status: `OPEN` (cheap, and it tests a prediction).
- **Five cheap items that block digits, not verdicts.** (i) **More seeds on the frontier** — P4/P5 are lower but not sd-separated, blocked entirely by **GDumb's variance** (~35 min/seed for the replay arms alone); it decides whether the supplementary claim is *"one budget point"* or *"a region"*. (ii) **The strict-φ cost at the 20 k arm** (measured only at 8 k; ~3.2 h) — it could shrink or grow, and it currently sits *on* the decision boundary. (iii) **More φ-fit compute** — NT-Xent is still falling at 20 k (3.98 vs a converged ~2.5–3) and a 50 k arm would likely put the gate **comfortably** rather than marginally above 0.35. (iv) **3-seed repeats at the ladder's top rung** (K=128 is 2 seeds; arm (b)'s K=64 FAIL is 1 seed + the atom re-check; the theorist's 31/32 is 1 seed per cell). (v) **The Stage-A fixed-radius control** (`mult = 4.0` at width 0.15 ⇒ radius 0.60) — one cell × 3 seeds, and it closes the factorial's only confound. Status: `OPEN`.
- **⚠ Two registered NOT-RUNs and one parked probe — recorded so none decays into a null.** (a) **The Split-CIFAR-10 entry run**: authorised by the cleared gate, **declined by the Head (2026-07-29)**, with its outcome **pre-registered in advance** (CLU **0.30–0.36**, `laundered = True`, above LwF/GDumb, near-or-below ER, below iCaRL, BWT far better than the parametric class) and its recipe, config block and ≈5 h cost handed over in full. Status: **`PARKED (registered-and-declined)`** — ⛔ **never reportable as a null.** (b) **The d = 8 K = 256 8×32 sharding probe (P4)**: launched as a cost probe and **deliberately killed** at machine load 139.76 before any arm completed; and it is **budget-confounded by construction** (N107: the atom floor is per-STORE ⇒ a parameter-matched 8-way split at d = 8 is ~8× starved). Status: **`PARKED`** — the analyst's recommendation is to fund the budget-adequate `sharded_per_shard` version on real hardware **or leave it registered-and-unrun**; ⛔ **do not re-attempt it parameter-matched.** (c) **The corruption level at which the τ regime flips** (somewhere between p = 0.5 and p = 0.8) has **never been swept**. Status: `OPEN`.
- **Harness/hygiene debts surfaced by w26 (none moves a published number, all are one-liners).** (i) The retry cell saves `mean_confidence` but **not `#{cos₀ < τ}`** — *the quantity that decides the entire τ question* — and the τ-sweep runs **only on the last corruption level**: **that pair is why a contradiction survived a wave.** (ii) `exp_cl_entry` nests the retry block inside `if "entry" in items`, so **`--items retry` silently produces an empty result** (the task file's own suggested command; use `--items entry,retry` or de-nest). (iii) `chlu exp-cl-entry --dataset cifar10` applies `apply_cifar10()` **after** the project YAML and silently overwrites `n_fit_region`/`n_fit_pool` — *a CIFAR run needing a non-default φ pool must set `dataset: cifar10` in the YAML and not pass `--dataset`.* (iv) **`results/` is untracked but not in `.gitignore`**, so any `git add -A` sweeps smoke artefacts into a commit (it caught one engineer). (v) `_canonical_sync` is a full **O(n) rebuild per op** (2.1× slower than relocate at n=8) — fine at MVC-0 sizes, needs the in-place slot move at thousands of items. Status: `OPEN` (all cheap; (i) is the one with a demonstrated cost).

**New three-wave fold (fourteen entries, schema-complete):**

- **⚠ Ratify the d=8 wording amendment — a one-line decision blocking a headline figure.** *What we haven't shown:* that `K_designed(8) = 1024` is **bracketed**. K=1024 passes at **0.9785**; **K=2048 was never run**, so the d=8 point is a *lower bound that coincides with* `4·2^8`. d=4 and d=6 **are** bracketed. *Why it matters:* the capacity law is one of the three things C1 hands to C2 as *"a law with a mechanism"*, and the difference between *"exact at three points"* and *"exact at two, lower-bounded at a third"* is exactly the kind of thing a referee checks. *Cheapest decisive cell:* either **ratify the wording** (free, Hub rec was *"YES, mechanical"*) **or run K=2048 at d=8** (expensive; the K=256 cell already cost **18 676 s** of write at d=6). Status: **`OPEN — NOT RULED (Head)`**; ⛔ until ruled, the approved wording is *"bracketed at d=4 and d=6, lower-bounded at d=8"* (**N123**).
- **⚠ The "certified" ruling — a three-way conflict between governing documents, 112 lines wide.** *What we haven't shown:* which of three incompatible rules governs. `deletion-prior-art` bans the word **program-wide**; **CM-22(m)** bans it only for **decay/eviction**; **Charter C-6 REQUIRES** the form *"certified within [stated scope]"* and **V1 §3 is built on it in a different technical sense** (LTT coverage certificates, `det J = 1` receipts). The writer found **112 lines across 10 files, none in a deletion sense**, and correctly **did not edit them**. *Why it matters:* the alternative to a ruling is a 112-line rewrite across four papers that would need a fresh V1 pass. *Recommendation on file (Hub + writer):* **narrow the ban to its referent** — forbidden for any statement about an item's lifetime, its removal, or an adversary's ability to detect it; permitted for symplectic/BIBO/LTT receipts **with the C-6 fine print adjacent**. Status: **`OPEN — NOT RULED (Head)`** (**N131**).
- **⚠ M1 — amortized delete cost vs n.** *What we haven't shown:* the shipped store's delete cost as a function of store size. `_canonical_sync` is a full **O(n)** rebuild per op (2.1× slower than relocate at n=8). *Why it matters:* **Ginart Def. A.5 is about amortized TIME** — so **no deletion-efficiency sentence may be written at all** until this exists, and Candidate 2 is un-nameable without it. *Spec:* the delete-cost curve vs n, against the flat-datastore row-delete as the trivial substitute; the in-place slot move is the obvious fix. Status: **`OPEN — NOT RULED (Head)`**; Hub rec was *"not in C1 — hand to C2 or shelve with Candidate 2."* (**N131**).
- **⭐⭐ Route 3 — the trajectory as an addressable medium (the register's new highest-value item).** *What we haven't shown:* whether distinct pieces of an item's information can live at distinct **strided points** of the write-shaped trajectory — slots carrying **`(q_t, p_t)` pairs** — with the settled point carrying the rest. *Why it matters:* it is charter §2.1 candidates **(c)+(d)** made concrete, it **composes with §A2.2** (γ/M-indexed trajectories selecting among slot streams in one well), and it is the only remaining route that is *not* refuted by the byte floor or the point-estimator diagnosis. **And §A8.1 reframed the measurement:** momentum at small t is **almost pure store** (`p_t ≈ −∫∇V dt`) while position at small t is almost pure query — so the design-deciding quantity is **store-attribution over time**, per slot, q and p scored **separately**. *Pre-registered gate (§A9.4):* stage 2 unlocks iff per-slot store-attributable discriminability clears the launch-noise floor **beyond 2 SE, ≥3 seeds, ≥1 family, at any t** — **a live p-channel unlocks even with a dead q-channel.** ⛔ **§A9.5 anti-lookup kill-condition is mandatory on every cell:** a **per-slot matched-bytes table launder**; if it reproduces the slotted read, Route 3 has degenerated into K time-indexed lookup tables and **fails regardless of dividend.** Status: **`TASKED (route3-stage1-plus-2x2`, C2W3)**; stage 2 **conditional**. ⛔ **A stage-1 failure is a clean pre-registered close — Route 3 is dead at this weight class and B′ absorbs the curve as protocol evidence.**
- **⭐⭐ The Route-1 × Route-2 2×2 — the wave's most informative unrun cell, flagged independently by both engineers.** *What we haven't shown:* Route 1's `λ_path` term applied to Route 2's shell basis. *Why it matters:* **`λ_path` is exactly the signal the shell radius is currently blind to** (N151) — the 2×2 is the direct test of whether failure mode (3), *the objective cannot see the design*, is **curable by making the objective see it.** *Cost:* hours. Both branches are merged, the seam takes `write_objective=…`, and the shell rig is orthogonal. Status: **`TASKED (route3-stage1-plus-2x2`, C2W3) — funded by §A9.1.**
- **⭐ A tilt direction CHOSEN to minimise the measured vacuum residual — the one probe that could rescue the pseudo-Goldstone instantiation.** *What we haven't shown:* whether the tilt fails because *tilting is wrong* or because *the tilt direction was designed rather than fitted*. C2W2 tilted along a **designed** direction and measured a residual of **0.140–0.343** against a random baseline of **0.167**. *Why it matters:* it is the exact w20-doctrine move — **designed mechanism, learned placement** — applied one level down, and it is the difference between *"§A4.2 is wrong"* and *"§A4.2 was instantiated wrong."* *Spec (the engineer's own):* pick `û` to minimise `Σ_j w_j (û·n̂_j)²` at each written site, then re-run the tilt sweep. Also unrun in the same family: **rank>1 tilts · per-atom tilt directions · a learned `ε`.** Status: **`OPEN`** — cheap, and the highest-value unrun cell in Route 2 (**N149**).
- **⭐ The factored / shared substrate — deferred, NOT closed, and it now carries a price tag written in advance.** *What we haven't shown:* whether sharing wells across items dissolves the **≥2.20×** byte floor and finally tests pillar (a) with converged writes. *Why it matters:* it is **the only known route** past N134, and §A4.5 called it *"the vision's core."* ⛔ **But §A9.9 binds any revival before it starts: deletion is measured as a CURVE** — exactness preserved on the private-atom fraction, measured degradation on the shared fraction — because **byte-exact deletion is never spent silently.** ⭐ **State the trade plainly: the property that makes deletion exact is the property that makes compression impossible.** Status: **`PARKED (deferred by §A11, not closed)`** — C2W3's headline was going to be this, and the gate re-scoped it.
- **⭐ `allocate` as a designed verb (v0) — specified, unbuilt.** *What we haven't shown:* a routing mechanism that decides *where* an item's information goes (simplex/discrete over endpoint dims · `(q,p)` slot pairs · particle attributes) with **a minimal learned policy trained through the trajectory read**. *Why it matters:* §A8.3 rules it **a doctrine-conformant extension, not a w20 defiance** — the verb set is the *designed* action space and was always meant to grow; only the policy is learned. ⛔ **Three collapse modes are declared in advance:** *allocation collapse* (all-endpoint = the status quo — **monitored, not forbidden; it is D2a from a new angle**), *leak-by-allocation* (early-t q-slots storing the query — guarded by the attribution curve), *ledger drift* (**allocation must never be a hidden capacity increase; the launder receives the same allocation budget**). Status: **`TASKED (route3-stage2`, C2W3, conditional on §A9.4)**; spec review owed by the theorist (§A11 task 5).
- **⭐ γ-as-selector — one pre-registered cell family, friction first.** *What we haven't shown:* same-well, **friction-indexed** trajectories read as distinct latents. *Why it matters:* §A2.2 is now **supported** (friction is the **~14× stronger** channel than mass), so *"mass as selector"* is live for the first time in 29 waves — and this is its cheapest instantiation. ⚠ **The band is bounded below by monitor #1** (`γ ≤ 0.03` trips on S0), which is precisely where the channel is strongest. Status: **`TASKED (route3-stage2`, C2W3, conditional)**; **mass second** (§A9.7).
- **⭐ The store-attribution curve — the measurement that replaced a rollout-length re-price.** *What we haven't shown:* per-slot dividend vs `t` (full − settle-deleted launder, **q and p separately, 3 seeds, laundered per slot**) on the existing merged rig. *Why it matters:* §A8.1 rules **length was the wrong variable to buy** — if the latent is the visited state itself, the store acts from the **first** step, and the question is *when* the trajectory is store-attributable rather than *how long* it is. ⭐ **It is also the fallback deliverable: if Route 3 dies, B′ absorbs the curve as protocol evidence.** Status: **`TASKED (route3-stage1-plus-2x2`, C2W3).**
- **⭐ "Encoder controls whether trajectories diverge or coincide" = supervising the flow map's Jacobian — stated, never built.** *What we haven't shown:* a write/encoder objective that makes the flow map **contractive within an item's launch cloud** (noise robustness) and **separated across items** (capacity) — *Lyapunov structure as the storage medium.* *Why it matters:* it is §A8.2, it is the only proposal on file that treats the *dynamics* rather than the *landscape* as the thing being written, and **both properties are measurable per slot, under launder.** ⚠ It is also the natural home for the write-objective redesign §A8.4 now permits (three invariants: coefficient-zero bit-identity · a liveness anchor · per-slot laundering). Status: **`THEORY-ONLY`** — no task, no owner, and the sharpest unbuilt idea in the C2 charter.
- **⭐ FB1 and the audit paper's own falsifiers — B′ can be killed, and by design.** *What we haven't shown:* whether **≥1 established paper already runs a matched-byte non-parametric control and reports the same verdict** — in which case **B′ is a replication, P4 is dead, and `bprime-rivals` is re-scoped rather than refined** (**FB1**). Likewise **FB4**: *if the +0 B substitute is at ceiling for every family including full attention, the protocol is measuring the task, not the memory.* *Why it matters:* the prereg was written **before** the gate was adjudicated, and it names its own kill conditions — that is what makes B′ a contribution rather than a retreat. Status: **`TASKED (bprime-fb1-recon`, `bprime-fb4-gate`, C2W3) — FB4 runs first** (**N166**).
- **⚠ The Track-2 real-data leg, and the published weakness it must survive.** *What we haven't shown:* a dividend on **enwik8 / WikiText-103 at 26–47 M params** — the **only** surviving Track-2 primary — against **three mandatory substitute columns INCLUDING dynamic evaluation.** *Why it matters:* **Krause et al. ICML'18 reach 1.08 bpc on Hutter Prize by gradient adaptation to recent history alone**, so ⛔ **if our dividend vanishes once dynamic evaluation is in the table, the primary is dead** — and we should say so before a referee does. The favourable half is real: the **+0 B pure-kNN read is degenerate** (λ=1 ⇒ ∞ perplexity) and PPM sits at **1.52–1.71 bpc** vs **1.232** for a 47 M AWD-LSTM, so the trivial substitutes do not own this metric. Status: **`PARKED — Head-reserved at the C2W3→C2W4 boundary, NO compute pre-committed`** (§A9.11; **N162**).
- **Harness / hygiene debts surfaced by C2W1 + C2W2 (none moves a published number; two are blocking).** ⛔ **(i) `store_write_mask_factory` (reconciliation 5) — BLOCKING any future store family**: `store_potential_factory` alone is insufficient because a new family cannot supply its own update mask, and an unmasked leaf **breaks C3 locality** (asserted in a test). One config field fixes it; it is a **prerequisite for the factored store**. `TASKED (bprime-fb4-gate` rider, C2W3). ⛔ **(ii) The `AttentionPsi` quarantine (reconciliation 1)** — until it lands, **no attention-ψ trajectory number is quotable store-relative** (`q0_only` **0.35–0.45** vs a 0.19 bar). `TASKED (psi_readout.py` rider, C2W3). **(iii) SC-1…SC-7 landing as a monitored soft constraint** (§A9.8) — ⚠ **and `B = 0.33` needs re-deriving on R1's corrected inradius before it is quoted as derived** (**N154**). **(iv) Monitors #10 and M14 have never fired anywhere** — untested, not green (**N142**). **(v) Eviction must RE-DRAW a freed atom group and `place` needs an INJECTIVITY guard** — both fixed in-code, both **generalise to anywhere a slot is recycled or a minimum is assumed unique** (**N146**). **(vi) A design-conformance test** — *before claiming a designed structure is in force on a learned store, measure whether some term in the loss reads it* (**N151**); no monitor currently catches this. Status: (i)/(ii) `TASKED`, the rest `OPEN`.

**New C2W3 fold (twenty entries, schema-complete):**

- ⭐⭐ **The tier-ii ORGANIZATION DIVIDEND — the cat test.** *What we haven't shown:* that a **physics-trained organization** of the latent space beats a **matched-capacity non-physics organizer** (gradient-trained placement / VQ / kNN / Titans-style write; **same φ, same bytes**) on **compositional / unseen queries** — e.g. an unseen *cat* query landing in wells shared with *dogs* (fur, four-legs) and far from *crow* wells (wings, feathers). *Why it matters:* ⭐⭐ **this is the axis 30 waves of launders never touched.** The settle-deleted launder gives both arms the **same placement**; it can only ever score the *read*. ⭐ **And it dissolves the program's most demoralising number: dividend ≈0 for a CLU that organizes well and then reads like a table is a FEATURE — an inference-cost win — not a failure.** *Its own control:* the **organizer swap** (⭐ **controls move up a level, they never relax**); inference reads explicitly **permitted** to be table-like. *Cheapest decisive cell:* the pre-registration first — metric, cat-test design, the non-physics organizer arms, the falsifiers. Status: **`TASKED (orgdiv-prereg`, C2W4) → the build is C2W5** (**N190**).
- ⭐⭐ **The tier-iii BLOCK on real streams** (⚠ *"CLU-former"* — ⛔ **PLACEHOLDER NAME, never in a draft**). *What we haven't shown:* the CLU as the **recurrent memory of a streaming block** (embedding + CLU + control + assimilation/decoder + norms + residual), chained, on **enwik8 / WT-103 at 26–47 M**, at matched params **AND** state-bytes. *Why it matters:* ⭐ **attention itself is a data-dependent table at inference — its value was only ever demonstrated in composition, inside a block.** We have never measured the CLU in composition. *Its own control:* the **SYSTEM-LEVEL SWAP** (a matched-state GRU/TTT-class cell in the same block) **plus dynamic evaluation as a mandatory substitute column**. ⛔ **Emergence claims live here and ONLY here**, with a **pre-committed directional falsifier written BEFORE the run**; *"needs >500 M to see"* is a **feasibility finding**, Head-owned. ⚠ **Design rules inherited from 29 waves:** the block trains end-to-end **only** through trajectory reads / implicit gradients (`∂q*/∂q₀ = 0` makes this MORE important in-system, not less); γ/M selectors only through that channel; allocation collapse is an attractor; the `2α` coercivity floor; the soft certificate as the sharing precondition. Status: **`TASKED (cluformer-pilot`, C2W4, conditional on the §A14.3 checkpoint)** (**N190**).
- ⭐⭐ **C6 — the third-party attribution probe, on the rig that already exists.** *What we haven't shown:* the measured **Δslot from deleting a stored item the query did NOT select**, as a curve vs `t` and vs `d/s`, across {**designed gate** `d/s = 4.4` · **soft-certificate region** `≈2.9` · **the override rig** `≈1.9–2.0`}. *Why it matters:* ⭐ **it is the ONLY coupling proven inexpressible by a per-slot table** (a table's output changes by **exactly 0** when a non-selected row is deleted), and ⛔ **our own admission gate suppresses it over a 1089× span** — **7.0e-4 / 0.111 / 0.69–0.80**. ⭐⭐ **It was measurable all of C2W3 at zero build cost and was not measured.** *Where it lands:* ⭐ **B′ protocol evidence — *"here is what a table structurally cannot do, measured"* is an audit-paper column — NOT a Route-3 revival** (§A14.1). *Riders:* price it with C2W2's `λ_min ÷ 2.2–6.0`; **and it is the cross-item successor's entry gate.** Status: **`TASKED (bprime-c6`, C2W4)** (**N180**).
- ⭐ **OQ-2 — a learned `p₀` steering the path toward NON-selected wells.** *What we haven't shown:* whether a live particle head can beat the `exp(−½(d/s)²)` suppression by steering. *Why it matters:* ⛔ **it is the ONLY pre-registered revival trigger for inference-read Route-3 claims (§A14.1), wave-boundary only** — and the theorist calls it *"the most promising thing stage 2 could try."* ⚠ **The 2.60-decade third-party scaling is a two-well toy at `p₀ = 0`; with a live head the suppression could be materially weaker.** Status: **`THEORY-ONLY` — no task, and it may not be opened mid-wave.**
- ⭐ **A CROSS-ITEM Route-3 successor.** *What we haven't shown:* any Route-3-shaped claim that is **cross-item** rather than per-item. *Why it matters:* ⛔ **per-item is closed by arithmetic** — rank **3d+1** for every `S ≥ 2` means a per-slot table can hold strictly *more* per-item content than the CLU can generate (**N174**) — so **cross-item is the only surviving shape**, and the only named cross-item mechanism is third-party attribution. *Where it lands:* gated on C6. Status: **`OPEN` — shape known, no design.**
- ⭐ **A replacement reader-discrimination family, built to §A3.7's four rules.** *What we haven't shown:* a second family satisfying *"the answer is provably not in the table"*. *Why it matters:* ⛔ **B′ currently runs six rivals against ONE surviving synthetic family, and that thinness goes in the paper's limitations verbatim** (§A14.2). *Cost:* ⚠ **a wave's work, not a rider.** *Decision point:* the **C2W4 review, with rival rows in hand.** Status: **`OPEN — decision deferred by §A14.2`** (**N171**).
- ⭐ **`null*` — the honest null over the launder's allocation class.** *What we haven't shown:* `null* = max_{a ∈ A} score(table with allocation a)` — ⭐ **computable, not estimated**, because `A` is small and discrete-or-simplex. *Why it matters:* ⛔ **handing the table ONE sampled allocation matches the *budget* but not the *search*** — the CLU's allocation is optimised, the table's is fixed. ⭐ **The strongest cheap version is the ORACLE-allocation table** (allocation chosen on the *test* split): a CLU that beats it is safe against any allocation policy anyone could have written. ⚠ **And the +0 B substitute audit must be re-run per allocation** — C2W1's audit went 0-for-4 precisely because substitutes were allowed information the frozen launder was not. Status: **`THEORY-ONLY` — a MANDATORY column for any future allocation claim** (bprime-theory T5.2).

Status: **`OPEN` — ⭐ arguably the highest-leverage unmeasured quantity in the C2 instrument** (**N181**).

The theorist proved **one exists** and proved **per-item slot richness is not a route**; ⛔ **he explicitly did not prove exhaustiveness.** *Why it matters:* the §A9.5 kill is *"no escape was measured"*, not *"no escape exists"* — and the difference is a referee question. Status: **`THEORY-ONLY`** (**N181**).
- **OQ-1 — the `{M, γ}` gradient prefactor.** *What we haven't shown:* whether `‖∂L/∂log M‖ ∝ N e^{−C(N)}` (the toy law) or `∝ e^{−C(N)}` (what the harness suggests — the point-arm mass gradient **8.73e-9** sits **`N ≈ 1200×` below** the toy's law while `‖∂L/∂φ‖` matches the `O(1)` law). *Why it matters:* ⭐ **the structural claim is unaffected either way** (exactly 0 implicit, exponentially small unrolled) — **but a published prefactor that is wrong by 1200× is a referee's free win.** *Cheapest decisive cell:* `exp_phi_particle --part grad` at `N ∈ {200, 400, 800, 1200}`, everything else fixed — **one flag, ~10 min.** Status: **`OPEN`** (**N181**).

Owed since C2W2.** Status: **`TASKED (bprime-c6`, C2W4)** (**N154/N182**).

### ⭐⭐ C2W4 FOLD — the wave that built the rival rows, tuned them against itself, and put the store inside a block (added 2026-08-01 by the `doc-curator` top-up, **[C2W4]**)

> **Context.** C2W4 ran **8 spokes over two review batches** and closed: `bprime-rivals` · `bprime-c6` · `harness-debt` · `orgdiv-prereg` · the C2W3 curator pass, then `bprime-rivals-f3` · `cluformer-pilot` · `bprime-draft` v1. **All 8 accepted.** The tried-and-failed half is `negative_results.md` **N191–N210** (+ the three discharged placeholders **N167 / N169 / N180**). ⚠ **Governance:** direction is set by `.claude/advisor-head-c2-charter.md` **ADDENDUM 3**; items below name their charter section or their §10 owner.
>
> ⭐⭐ **THE STRUCTURAL CHANGE THIS FOLD RECORDS: the register's centre of gravity moved from "what have we not measured?" to "what have we not BUILT?".** Tier i is now measured to its own ceiling — the audit survives tuning and the answer is uniformly negative at matched bytes. **Tier iii ran and produced a MECHANISM, not a score: the store is inert inside the block, and the named cause (atom placement at init) is a BUILD, not an experiment.** ⭐ **So for the first time in this register the highest-value item is a three-hour local probe, not a cluster run** — and the Hub endorsed exactly that sequencing.

**New C2W4 fold (eighteen entries, schema-complete):**

- ⭐⭐ **THE PLACEMENT PROBE — the cheapest decisive experiment the register currently holds.** *What we haven't shown:* whether the block's store is inert because **128 atoms per group are initialised SCATTERED at scale 1.0** (`atom_local_radius = 0.0`, the historical default) in a `dim = 3` ball, so a few unrolled steps cannot gather them into a well at the target. *Why it matters:* ⭐⭐ **it is the named cause of the wave's first-order negative (N196), it costs hours locally, and running 26–47 M on CSF3 before it would spend ~108 A100-hours reproducing a mechanism already isolated at 0.16 M for 1.35 CPU-hours.** ⚠ **And it doubles as tier-ii groundwork — the write objective IS the organizer.** *Cheapest decisive cell:* `atom_local_radius` at its designed band (the N98 localized init, **a shipped lever**), and/or a **trajectory term in the write objective**. *Where it lands:* gates S4. Status: **`OPEN — Hub-recommended, Head decision 1 at the C2W4 second review`** (**N196**).
- ⛔ **S4 — the 26–47 M CSF3 run itself.** *What we haven't shown:* the block at **26–47 M on enwik8** with the system-level swap and dyn-eval as a mandatory column. *Why it matters:* **DF1/DF2/DF3 are all NOT SCORED without it, and *"needs > 500 M"* is unavailable by pre-commitment until DF2 exists.** *State:* `scripts/csf3/job_gpu_cluformer.sh` is written, `bash -n` clean, **UNTESTED ON CLUSTER**; **0 of ≤108 A100-hours spent**; the blocker is **DNS/VPN from the agent, not the code**. ⚠ **The PREREG requires reporting the resolved JAX version on BOTH machines — only the local one (0.9.0) exists, so parity is EXPECTED, not VERIFIED.** Status: **`TASKED-BUT-UNRUNNABLE-BY-AGENT — Head-submittable`** (**N197**).
- ⛔ **The plan-pass vectorisation.** *What we haven't shown:* the block running with a controller loop that is not pure Python. *Why it matters:* ⭐ **77–84 % of the CLU arm's wall clock is the concrete plan pass, not physics** — netting it out, the CLU is ~37× the GRU on physics alone rather than 14–16× end-to-end — and **at pilot scale (96 lanes × 16 chunks) it is ~2 s/step of pure Python before a single Verlet step.** ⛔ **The GPU will idle behind it.** *Where it lands:* a prerequisite for any pilot run. Status: **`OPEN — C2W5 rider`** (`cluformer-pilot` §8.2).
- ⚠ **DECISION REPLAY — ratify or replace.** *What we haven't shown:* that a reviewer will accept *"the C2W1 controller's decisions are computed concretely in pass 1 and replayed differentiably in pass 2."* *Why it matters:* ⭐ **the whole tier-iii build rests on it**; nothing about the controller is weakened and `∂(decision)/∂θ` is **zero anyway** (the verbs are discrete, and T3's corollary calls it a gradient *attractor*), **but the fallback — a differentiable relaxation of admission — would be a DIFFERENT and WEAKER claim about the C2W1 controller.** *Where it lands:* a Hub/Advisor ruling **before** the pilot run, not after. Status: **`OPEN — C2W5 ratification item`** (Head decision queue 3, C2W4 second review).
- ⭐ **A tier-ii read that is not a settled point.** *What we haven't shown:* the **multi-particle occupancy** read the tier-ii prereg makes mandatory — it exists on paper and nowhere else. *Why it matters:* ⛔ **O1 proves a settled-point read is reproducible by an `N_min`-row table for EVERY reader, so composition cannot live in one** — the read is not a preference, it is a precondition. Status: **`THEORY-ONLY — registered, unbuilt`** (**N206**).
- ⛔ **The tier-ii dividend measured OFF the training support.** *What we haven't shown:* any score on **unseen combinations**. *Why it matters:* the only usable non-VQ channel (the **inertial** term, 19–20 % of query mass) is **coherent in distribution** (1-NN recovery 0.989–1.000), so ⭐ **in-distribution scoring is structurally incapable of showing the dividend — "score it on unseen combinations" is a DERIVATION, not a preference.** ⚠ *Rider:* the honest window `γ ∈ [0.05, 0.1]` **collides with monitor #1's hot low end** (`γ ≤ 0.03` trips on S0) — the band is narrow on both sides. Status: **`TASKED (C2W5)`** (**N207**).
- ⭐ **The rescue gate, rebuilt so it is not a coin flip.** *What we haven't shown:* a rival-rescue criterion whose variance is smaller than the lift it gates. *Why it matters:* ⛔ **at n = 3 the gate flipped three arms and retracted one of our own never-quotes** (**N193**). *Three named fixes, none built:* **(a)** quote the n = 9 column; **(b)** make the blank a **PAIRED per-seed** statistic; **(c)** average the blank over several inits. Status: **`OPEN — any future rival work runs ≥ 9 seeds or pairs the control`**.
- ⭐ **The full audit column set at n = 9.** *What we haven't shown:* null / launder / ledger aggregated at 9 seeds — only the `f3` and C2W4-code **margin** columns exist at that power. *Cost:* **a 10-minute re-aggregation.** *Why it matters:* the draft's table would otherwise mix powers across columns. Status: **`OPEN — Hub call before the freeze`**.
- ⭐ **Held-out best-of-grid as the STANDING selection rule.** *What we haven't shown:* whether F3's rule should change program-wide. *Why it matters:* ⛔ **selecting on the fit objective makes the `wd` axis unselectable (12/45 tie-breaks) and the low-lr half never chosen (0/45); under a held-out split the added points ARE chosen (26/45, 24/45)** ⇒ **the grid is operationally 6×1 unless the rule changes too.** *State:* **implemented behind `--n-val` / `select_on="val"`**, reported as a declared secondary. Status: **`OPEN — adopting it as primary is a Hub decision`** (**N194**).
- ⛔ **The `overload` frontier column under F3.** *What we haven't shown:* the byte-frontier row re-scored under the tuned grid. *Why it matters:* if the Hub adopts the n = 9 rescue verdicts, the trigger condition (*"the grid rescues an arm at `aggregate`"*) is **arguably met for `deltanet` — but by POWER, not by the grid**, so the engineer correctly did not run it unilaterally. *Cost:* **one labelled row, ~5 min.** Status: **`OPEN — declared NOT-RUN, never a null`**.
- ⛔ **`ttt_linear`'s rescue status.** *What we haven't shown:* which side of the gate it is on — it is **rescued under one init scheme and not the other** (lift 0.09–0.32 either way) at n = 9. *Why it matters:* ⛔ **no margin against it is quotable while it is unpinned**, and it is one of only five rival rows. *Cheapest decisive cell:* the paired-blank fix above, or more seeds. Status: **`OPEN`** (**N193**).
- ⛔ **Monitor #11's zero-depth guard, in `monitors.py` where it belongs.** *What we haven't shown:* `saddle_reach_threshold` surviving `D → 0` (`2αs² == 0.0` ⇒ `ZeroDivisionError`; **it killed a seed**). *State:* **guarded in the pilot's caller only** — the file was read-only to that task. ⭐ *Semantics to preserve:* **an item with no well is ABSENT, not UNREACHABLE.** Status: **`OPEN — C2W5 engineer owning `monitors.py``** (C2W4 reconciliation 1).
- ⛔ **Monitor #6 in-block, and monitor #2's domain guard (D5).** *What we haven't shown:* **#6 never became APPLICABLE in the block** (three observations through one persistent registry is short of its window — *a longer run or a smaller `monitor_every` fixes it cheaply*); and **D5 — the `λ_min ≤ 0` domain guard for monitor #2 — is still NOT RUN**, because `r_i` is computed at `chlu/core/clu_system.py:1309`, which no C2W4 task owned. ⚠ **The silent `max(λ, 1e-9)` clamp at `clu_system.py:906` must be adjudicated in the same task.** Status: **`OPEN — needs an owner who holds `clu_system.py`, sequenced after `bprime-c6``**.
- ⛔ **Re-locating `B` on the SHIPPED 4-D store.** *What we haven't shown:* the capture-radius leg run where the certificate actually lives — D3's grid is the theorist's **2-D toy**, exactly as the original `B = 0.33` was. *Why it matters:* ⛔ **`B` still may not be quoted as derived, and now for a stronger reason: its edge is not located at all.** ⚠ **And R1's corrected inradius does not transfer to the anisotropic (`naxis`) store the certificate work uses** — a second unshown quantity in the same place. Status: **`OPEN — `monitors.py` / `clu_system.py` territory`** (**N205**).
- ⭐ **The φ-gain calibration as a program-wide anti-collapse initialisation.** *What we haven't shown:* whether *"set the gain so the RMS address norm equals the store's `ball_radius`"* is the right default beyond one venue. *Why it matters:* ⭐ **at gain 1.0 the store starts life refusing 60/64 offers — the degenerate corner, reached at STEP 0** (a `tanh` MLP on LayerNormed pooled summaries emits RMS ≈ 0.125 at `d_model = 64`, far inside the merge radius); the rule is **principled, scale-free and identical in every arm** (measured gains 3.839 / 6.356 / 5.650, refusals down to 16–22/64). ⚠ **And the venue property it exposed: enwik8 chunk summaries are NOT naturally `d_safe`-separated in the store's address ball** (pairwise separation ~0.04 against `d_safe = 0.389`). Status: **`OPEN — declared for any streaming venue`**.
- ⚠ **WikiText-103, and word-level anything.** *What we haven't shown:* the block on any venue but byte-level enwik8. *State:* **the loader is BUILT and NOT RUN** (`chlu/data/wikitext.py`, byte + word level, vocabulary from **train only**); byte-level enwik8 was chosen deliberately to remove the tokenizer as a confound. Status: **`OPEN — built, not the venue`**.
- ⛔ **The three §5.3 candidate fixes, as a comparison rather than a choice.** *What we haven't shown:* which of **`atom_local_radius > 0`** / **a payload-carrying ψ residual** / **a trajectory term in the write objective** actually restores acquisition — and whether any of them survives the byte ledger. *Why it matters:* ⭐ **all three are DESIGN changes, not knob turns, and §8.2 forbade building them mid-run to obtain a clean number** — which is why they are here rather than in the report. Status: **`OPEN — the tier-iii critical path`** (**N196**).
- ⚠ **The reversal-sweep discipline, as a standing process item.** *What we haven't shown:* a mechanism that propagates a reversed Head ruling into **every derived document an agent actually reads** (agent briefs, the claims matrix's own sections, task templates) rather than only the charter and the handover. *Why it matters:* ⛔ **a reversed C-1 lived in the paper-writer brief for three weeks and in `claims_matrix.md` §3 for longer** — caught only because a spoke followed the Charter over its own instructions. Status: **`OPEN — Hub/Head process item`** (**N210**).
  - > ⛔⛔ **STATUS SHARPENED (2026-08-01, `doc-curator-c2w4-fold`): the sweep's first execution MISSED A SITE, which is this item's own thesis demonstrated twice.** ✅ `.claude/agents/paper-writer.md` is fixed and dated (A18.2 discharged there); ⛔ **`.claude/agents/paper-writer 2.md` — a duplicate declaring the SAME `name: paper-writer` — still carries the pre-reversal C-1.** ⚠ **Seven `<agent> 2.md` duplicates exist and the set is NOT uniformly stale** (the `doc-curator` pair matches) ⇒ ⭐ **the unshown mechanism is now specific: enumerate every file an agent can LOAD (duplicates, backups, same-`name:` collisions) as part of the sweep.** Status: **`OPEN — Hub/Head process item, one site still live`** (**N210** addendum).

**New boundaries this scoping surfaced (four, schema-complete):**

- ⛔⛔ **A BYTE-MATCHED TIER-ii RESULT — declared out of scope BEFORE the tier runs.** *What we haven't shown:* any tier-ii organization dividend at **matched bytes**. *Why it matters:* ⭐ **the audit paper's whole discipline is matched bytes, so a reviewer will ask it of tier ii on sight** — and the answer is pre-registered: **`PREREG-TierII.md` §5.2 makes no byte-matched tier-ii promise, because reaching it needs `K ≈ 3.6k`, which is a WAVE OF ITS OWN.** ⚠ **The design point reports a ratio of `5.00×` and never CLAIMS it** (`N_a = 32, F = 4, K = 128, a = 12`; ⛔ `N_a = 16` FORBIDDEN, **N208**). *Where it lands:* tier-ii paper future-work; a future wave. Status: **`OPEN — declared scope limit, not a null`** (C2W5 scoping, Hub action 2).
- ⚠ **THE POST-C2W5 SHORTS RE-PASS (A18.7), and the "CLU" name debut inside it.** *What we haven't shown:* the program's shorts polished to submission-ready with the **latest results considered for each short's main text or appendix** — and, separately, ⛔ **what the primitive is actually CALLED in print** (*"CLU-former" is a placeholder everywhere; the name-debut question was absorbed into this pass*). *Why it matters:* ⭐ **the re-pass is the declared focus WHILE CSF3 is in flight** — it is the program's next non-experimental block of work, and every short is currently frozen against registries that have moved three waves. *Where it lands:* every short; the Head owns venue and timing exclusively. Status: **`OPEN — sequenced after C2W5 (A18.7), Head-owned`**.
- ⚠ **THE `s`-ESTIMATOR AS A BLOCKING FIRST-DAY INSTRUMENT — and what happens if it disagrees with 0.40.** *What we haven't shown:* an **effective-`s` estimator** validated on the factored store the cat test builds. *Why it matters:* ⛔ **the entire tier-ii operating band is stated in units of `s` (`d/s ∈ [2.5, 2.9]`, the soft-certificate band), and `s` is already known NOT to be a constant of the architecture (0.482 → 0.362 across a sweep, N180/N181)** ⇒ **if the factored store's effective `s` lands elsewhere, the band moves with it and the cat test's claim cells move too.** ⭐ **It is scoped as day-one BLOCKING (prereg OQ-1), which is the right shape** — but *"the estimator agrees with the shipped store's 0.40"* is an assumption, not a measurement. *Where it lands:* `orgdiv-cat-test`; tier-ii methods. Status: **`TASKED (C2W5) — blocking, and its disagreement case is unplanned`**.
- ⚠ **A TIER-iii DRAFTING ANSWER TO THE DECISION-REPLAY OBJECTION — ruled, but not written.** *What we haven't shown:* the **prose** that answers *"the controller's decisions are detached"* for a referee. *Why it matters:* ⭐ **the ratification (decision 1) closed the DESIGN question and explicitly deferred the RHETORICAL one to tier-iii drafting** — so the risk is not retired, it is scheduled. ⛔ **And the fallback is now unavailable by ruling** (the differentiable-admission relaxation is NOT scoped), which means the prose has to carry it alone. *Where it lands:* the tier-iii paper's methods. Status: **`OPEN — deferred to tier-iii drafting by ruling`**.

### ⭐⭐ C2W5 FOLD — the wave that refuted a READ PROTOCOL and found the family solvable (added 2026-08-01 by `doc-curator-c2w5-fold`, **[C2W5]**)

> **Context.** C2W5 ran **10 spokes over two review batches**, all accepted: `orgdiv-cat-test` · `pilot-placement-probe` · `bprime-referee` · `bprime-cite-check` · `doc-curator-c2w4-fold`, then `orgdiv-null-arms` · `bprime-referee-closures` · `bprime-mamba2-arm` · `plan-pass-vectorise` · `bprime-draft-r3`. The tried-and-failed half is `negative_results.md` **N212–N224** (+ dated addenda on N193 / N196 / N204 / N211). Direction is set by charter **ADDENDUM 5 §A20** (filed 2026-08-01, binding).
>
> ⛔⛔ **THE RE-LABELLING GOVERNS EVERY LINE BELOW (§A20.2): the refuted object is "the `P`-particle occupancy read protocol at `P = 4`", NEVER "the compositional family".** ⛔ **And 0.272 is a *reference ceiling from an out-of-class decoder* — what the launch information supports, never an arm's bar.**
>
> ⭐⭐ **THE STRUCTURAL CHANGE THIS FOLD RECORDS: the register's tier-ii items moved from "is this family learnable?" to "what read can express the answer?".** The family is **solvable from the very inputs every arm saw** (27.2 % exact as launched, 100 % noiseless); what does not exist is a read protocol that can occupy `F` distinct wells and decode a continuous coordinate. ⭐ **So the highest-value tier-ii items below are all READ-side builds, and one of them (`K0`) is a free pre-condition that costs seconds and would have priced this whole wave in advance.**

**Status moves on the C2W4 + C2W5-scoping folds (provenance kept — ⛔ nothing deleted):**

| earlier item | disposition after C2W5 |
|---|---|
| ⭐⭐ **The placement probe** *(was `TASKED (C2W5 pilot-placement-probe)`)* | **→ SHOWN (C2W5) — and both of its ranked hypotheses are REFUTED.** ⛔ **H1 (`atom_local_radius`) is worse than doing nothing** (up to **−6 orders** of depth at its designed band; the write site is controller-chosen and unknown at init); ⛔ **H2 (`write_lambda_traj`) is bit-identically inert at 3.9× wall clock** (gradient ~1e-13 at flat init; sign-SGD needs a sign flip). ⭐ **What wakes depth is placement-at-write × hinge margin (0.0288 → 0.1668)** — ⛔ **but margin 1.0 collapses between-item `q*` discrimination (0.114 → 0.054).** Status: **`→ SHOWN (C2W5, pilot-placement-probe) — hypotheses refuted, route RE-PRICED not closed`** (**N221**). |
| ⛔ **The three §5.3 candidate fixes, as a comparison rather than a choice** *(was `OPEN — the tier-iii critical path`)* | ⭐⭐ **RANKED BY MEASUREMENT, and the survivor was ranked LAST: the ψ payload residual.** The dynamics deliver **30–50 %** of the payload into `q*` (blank: exactly 0.000) and **ψ compresses it 7–25×** (decoded range 0.0085–0.0117 vs true 0.2277; between-item decode spread ≈ the live-vs-blank difference). **New boundaries this wave surfaced (thirteen, schema-complete):**

- ⭐⭐ **`K0` — THE PRE-CONDITION THAT COSTS SECONDS AND WOULD HAVE PRICED THE WHOLE WAVE.** *What we haven't shown:* `K0` applied **before** a store is written, on any future compositional cell. *Why it matters:* ⭐ **`P(≥ F distinct wells occupied) = 0.050` is computable from the LAUNCH GEOMETRY ALONE, store-free, in seconds** — it caps every arm at 0.05 before any organizer exists, and nobody computed it until after the wave. *Where it lands:* **Hub-ADOPTED as a pre-condition beside K1–K5**; tier-ii methods; every future cat-test-shaped design. Status: **`ADOPTED (doctrine) · UNAPPLIED (no future cell exists yet)`** (**N214**).
- ⭐⭐ **A READ PROTOCOL THAT CAN EXPRESS MULTI-WELL ANSWERS — replaced, not patched (§A20.3(b)).** *What we haven't shown:* any read that occupies `F` distinct wells **and** decodes a *continuous* code rather than quantising to one codebook entry per particle. *Why it matters:* ⛔ **quantisation is the named destructive step — N1/N2/N3 all replace the launch point with a codebook centre, discarding exactly the coordinate the 27.2 % reference decoder uses.** *Where it lands:* `tierii-read-fix` (wt2, **spawns only on Head GO after task-file review**). Status: **`TASKED-PENDING-GO`** (**N214/N215**).
- ⭐ **THE k-PARTICLE LEARNED-LAUNCH HEAD, WITH ITS FOUR BLOCKING GUARDS (§A20.3(c)).** *What we haven't shown:* an encoder that emits **k full particles** (k `q₀`, masses, frictions) with **overlap-as-confidence** (confident ⇒ k collapse to `F` unique; unfamiliar ⇒ k scattered guesses) and **dedupe/evolve-unique as a controller verb doubling as the compute dial**. *The four guards, all from banked measurements:* **(i)** the launch-only launder — ⚠ **Advisor amendment A1, must-fix: it is RECOMPUTED LIVE on the task's own learned launches (store deleted, same reader class, same k) and the full read must beat THAT; 0.272 is a reference line only, never the live bar** (a strong launch head could clear a stale bar with the store deleted); **(ii)** a **soft-occupancy** training signal (hard assignments don't backprop; placement gradients are dead until wells exist, **1e-10 → O(1)**); **(iii)** staged store-then-launch **co-training** (w20); **(iv)** **k on the byte ledger, all arms matched**. Status: **`TASKED-PENDING-GO — Advisor GO on two amendments, both folded`**.
- ⭐ **CONSOLIDATE-TO-BUDGET + TRASH-REGION PRUNING — `γ_φ(q)`'s FIRST USE (§A20.3(d)).** *What we haven't shown:* over-dig freely → **merge to the designed well budget** (mechanical, measurable criteria) → **prune BELOW budget as a controller decision**, pruned wells routed to the trash region. *Why it matters:* ⭐ **`γ_φ(q)` has been built since w3 and has never been used for anything**; and the discipline it enforces is *spurious shallow wells are TRASHED, never merged into meaningful ones*. Status: **`TASKED-PENDING-GO`**.
- ⭐ **LEARNED `p₀` AS A REGISTERED LEVER (§A20.3(e)).** *What we haven't shown:* ballistic reach **across informationally dead inter-well gaps** — the gaps carry exponentially dead atom gradients plus only the confinement bowl, so no gradient-following read can cross them. *Why it matters:* it complements wormholes and it is the one lever that attacks *reach* rather than *depth*. Status: **`TASKED-PENDING-GO`**.
- ⭐ **THE `(d, P, launch_radius)` JOINT SWEEP — the cheapest next instrument in the register, and it needs no store.** *What we haven't shown:* how the occupancy statistics (distinct wells, `≥ F` fraction, precision, ceiling) move jointly with address dimension, particle count and launch radius. *Why it matters:* ⭐ **every quantity in it is computable from launch geometry — it is `K0` turned into a design map**, and the `P`-sweep already shows the two axes separate (`P` fixes addressability, **not** precision: 0.40 at every `P`). *Where it lands:* tier-ii design; `orgdiv-null-arms` follow-up 3. Status: **`OPEN — declared NOT-RUN, cheapest decisive instrument`**.
- ⛔ **`P > 4` AS A MATCHED ARM.** *What we haven't shown:* a scored arm at higher fan-out. *Why it matters:* the sweep says `P = 64` lifts the reference ceiling to **0.938** and distinct wells to **4.34** — ⛔ **but exact-set occupancy is still 0.0016, so `P` alone is not the fix.** *State:* changing `P` re-draws the launch offsets ⇒ **a matched `P = 16` arm needs a NEW frozen φ and a NEW cat-test cell.** Status: **`OPEN — out-of-protocol diagnostic only, never a score`** (**N214**).
- ⛔ **A TUNED PHYSICS ARM, AND THE ORGANIZER SWAP AGAINST THE NEW READ.** *What we haven't shown:* the tier-ii control the tier was designed around — ⛔ **it has never been run, in either wave.** *Why it matters:* **the physics is NOT adjudicated** (§A20.1); the wave killed a read protocol, not a hypothesis about physics-trained organization. *Five banked design inputs for the next iteration:* address `F` distinct wells · keep the continuous coordinate · the ψ payload residual · the `S_eff` collapse fix · `m = 8 / a = 32`. Status: **`DEFERRED, NOT CANCELLED — the swap re-runs against the new read, at F3-equal tuning both ways`** (**N212**).
- ⛔ **THE `S_eff` COLLAPSE FIX.** *What we haven't shown:* a physics arm that runs **inside** its own registered band. *Numbers:* `S_eff` **34.1 / 51.2 / 36.6** against **[8, 16]**, 3/3 seeds — **rule-3 COLLAPSE.** *Why it matters:* ⛔ **every cat-test verdict is a reading of a collapsed, untuned arm**, which is exactly why the kill is quotable only in its "untuned and collapsed" form. Status: **`OPEN — a named design input, unbuilt`** (**N212**).
- ⛔ **THE §7.27 CAUSE — φ-DRIFT vs AMPLITUDE SUPPRESSION.** *What we haven't shown:* which of the two destroys the wells through outer training (**0.029 → 4.95e-63**). *State:* ✅ **an instrument now exists and ships on by default** (`store_health_probe`), with a pre-registered CSF3 abort criterion — ⛔ **but the single gradient probe that would separate the two mechanisms is unrun and unowned.** Status: **`OPEN — instrumented, uncaused`** (**N223**).
- ⚠ **A DEPTH-VS-DISCRIMINATION OBJECTIVE.** *What we haven't shown:* a write objective that deepens a well **without** collapsing between-item discrimination. *Why it matters:* ⛔ **measured, they trade: margin 1.0 takes depth 0.0288 → 0.1668 and `q*` discrimination 0.114 → 0.054** — *a deeper well at a shared payload location is a WORSE memory.* Status: **`OPEN — the shipped `write_margin = 0.15` is a ceiling that is also a protection`** (**N221**).
- ⛔⛔ **THE §A20.2 RE-LABELLING SWEEP HAS A LIVE SITE IN TRACKED CODE — and no curator can close it.** *What we haven't shown:* the sweep finished. ⛔ **`chlu/experiments/exp_null_arms.py` line 13 (module docstring) reads *"none clears ⇒ the family is refuted for every organizer class measured"* — the exact form §A20.2 forbids, in the one place the curator may not edit.** *Why it matters:* ⭐⭐ **this is N210's mechanism a third time — the sweep runs against the documents and the filesystem, and now it must also run against the CODE.** *Where it lands:* an engineer with `chlu/experiments/` in scope, one docstring line. Status: **`OPEN — flagged to the Hub, owner needed`** (**N210/N214**).
- ⚠ **THE `null*` ORACLE-BOUND CONVENTION, AND THE VALIDATION-GRAIN PROBLEM UNDER IT.** *What we haven't shown:* a selection statistic with enough grain to resolve anything on this family. *Why it matters:* ⛔ **with 32 validation rows the accuracy grain is 1/32 = 0.031, so EVERY config scored `val_acc = 0.0000` and selection ran entirely on the MSE tie-break** — the verdict survives only because the `Q_unseen` grid-max (grain 1/2560) was run as an **oracle-selected upper bound**. ⭐ **The transferable requirement: a seen-validation split must inherit the family's own held-out rule** (achieved here: `|A_val ∩ A_train| ≤ F−2` against **every** retained training row, 32/32 on all 5 seeds), **and it must have grain finer than the effect.** Status: **`OPEN — a protocol requirement with no home but this one`** (**N215**).

### ⭐⭐ C2W6 FOLD — the anti-erosion wave: the premise retired, the shield shipped, and a gradient channel nobody had named (added 2026-08-05 by `doc-curator-c2w6-fold`, **[C2W6]**)

> **Context.** C2W6 ran **two spokes**: `c2w6-anti-erosion` (engineer, **ACCEPTED**; branch merged `main @ d1149a4`, suite **1397/0**) and `c2w6-erosion-adjudication` (analyst, returned **`BLOCKED`** correctly and re-spawned). The tried-and-failed half is `negative_results.md` **N225–N230** (+ dated addenda on **N221 / N222 / N223 / N224**). Direction is set by charter **ADDENDUM 7 §A22–§A23** (Head-ratified 2026-08-05, binding). ⛔ **Everything is TOY (0.16 M, 3 paired seeds); every `w4` reading is N94-demoted, the `w40` pair is not.**
>
> ⛔⛔ **TWO RULINGS GOVERN EVERY LINE BELOW.** **(§A23.4)** *"4.95e-63"* is a **CENSORING STATEMENT**, never a point estimate — the approved form is *"200 outer steps drive well depth below 1e-62 on 3/3 seeds (1.4e-87 / 1.5e-62 / 2.5e-177)."* **(§A23.3)** every *"the write cannot train φ"* statement needs the **placement qualifier** (27 % of φ's layer-0 gradient flows through the write whenever `atom_place_radius > 0`).
>
> ⭐⭐ **THE STRUCTURAL CHANGE THIS FOLD RECORDS: the tier-iii lifetime question moved from *"how do we stop the optimizer destroying the store?"* to *"why would an optimizer maintain a store that costs more than it returns?"*** The destruction the register has been carrying since C2W5 **does not reproduce at the configuration we ship** — it survives only where the store is *useless* to the loss. ⇒ **the highest-value items below are about MAKING THE STORE PAY, not about protecting it.**
>
> ⛔⛔ **DATED FOLD-2 CORRECTION TO THIS BANNER (2026-08-06, `doc-curator-c2w6-fold-2`; Add.9 §A27.1 — the banner above is NOT rewritten).** The sentence *"the destruction … **does not reproduce at the configuration we ship**"* is **SUPERSEDED.** ✅ **It does reproduce — transiently.** At the run-2 config **N223's mechanism FIRES but is NOT TERMINAL**: depth falls to **0.9 % (step 150) / 5.3 % (step 275)** of untrained on 2/3 seeds (**112× / 19×** losses vs **1.05–1.33×** reading noise), then partially recovers (final/min **geo 4.20×**); ⭐⭐ **the banked N223 anchor was taken at 200 steps — INSIDE the trough — so E1's `final ÷ step-200` was structurally measuring RECOVERY, not decay.** ⛔⛔ **NEVER-QUOTE: *"the premise is retired"* AND *"N223's monotone collapse does not reproduce at the run-2 config"* (the latter was this fold's own recommended replacement and is now forbidden too).** ⛔ **No verdict moves: E1 is REFUTED 0/3 raw AND decay-netted.** ⭐ **The framing consequence for this section: the fold's thesis is UNCHANGED and if anything strengthened — the estimator-robust survivor is still the comparative claim *"erosion tracks uselessness"* (residual-off geo 0.0775×, −3.10 SE, vs run-2 geo 0.327×, n.s.) — but *"protecting the store"* is back on the table as live work, because the mechanism is live and P1 does not prevent the transient.** ⚠ **Also: propagation (B)'s sweep is CLOSED (see the φ-qualifier item below), and it carries a standing consequence — `settled_point` is a zero-gradient control ONLY at radius 0, and every banked `gradient_probe` ratio at the shipped config is a LOWER BOUND on the read's share (conservative, never inflated); ⛔⛔ the T3 bitwise zeros N135/N152/N201 are NOT affected.**

**New boundaries this wave surfaced (nine, schema-complete):**

- ⭐⭐ **DOES THE NON-REPRODUCTION SURVIVE SCALE AND HORIZON?** *What we haven't shown:* that *"N223's monotone collapse does not reproduce"* holds anywhere except **TOY (0.16 M), 1000 outer steps, 3 seeds, 409 600–2 M tokens**. *Why it matters:* ⛔ **the whole retirement of the wave's premise rests on a toy measurement**, and the CSF3 runs are the only place the question is real — while **the abort criterion that would have caught a scale collapse is now suspended**, so a scale collapse would be discovered post-hoc, in telemetry, after the allocation burned. *Where it lands:* CSF3 run-2/run-3 post-hoc depth telemetry; the tier-iii write-up's scope paragraph. Status: **`OPEN — the highest-leverage unpaid check in this fold`** (**N223** addendum / **N227**).
  - ⭐⭐ **RE-FRAMED 2026-08-06 (fold-2) — THE QUESTION IS NOW SHARPER AND MORE URGENT, NOT LESS.** ⛔ **The premise of this item is superseded: there IS no "non-reproduction" to survive scale.** At the run-2 config **N223's mechanism FIRES but is NOT TERMINAL** — a **transient trough** to **0.9 % / 5.3 %** of untrained at steps **150 / 275** on 2/3 seeds (112× / 19× losses), then partial recovery (final/min **geo 4.20×**). ⇒ ⭐⭐ **the open question is no longer *"does the absence of erosion survive scale?"* but *"does the RECOVERY survive scale and horizon?"*** — a strictly harder and more consequential question, because **a transient at toy scale and 1000 steps could be a terminal collapse at 26–47 M and a longer horizon, and nothing would stop it.** ⛔⛔ **The exposure is now WORSE than this item first recorded it:** the abort criterion is **SUSPENDED**, the trough is **exactly the shape that suspension was justified by**, and **P1 does NOT prevent the transient** (`p1_on` min/untrained geo **0.105**, troughs at 150/725/375) — so **the shield that ships does not close this risk either.** ⚠ **And any post-hoc read of CSF3 telemetry must fix its reference reading OUTSIDE the transient** (`final ÷ max over run`, or a registered late-window baseline): ⛔ **a step-200 denominator is config-coupled to the trough and will mis-report recovery as health.** ⚠ **Standing exposure the analyst could not close and neither can this register: every depth claim in C2W6 rests on 3 seeds with within-arm spreads of 13.7× (untrained depth 0.046 / 0.162 / 0.249) — no 2× depth effect is resolvable at n = 3; only sign consistency is.** Status: **`OPEN — RE-SCOPED and now the highest-leverage unpaid check in the program's lifetime story`** (**N223** supersession block / **N227**).
- ⭐⭐ **WHAT *SHOULD* TRIGGER AN ESCALATION, NOW THAT NOTHING DOES.** *What we haven't shown:* a destruction detector that does not fire on healthy runs. *Why it matters:* the pre-registered rule fires on **2 of 3 healthy seeds** at this config (dip-and-recover: 4.12e-4 → 15.2× recovery), so it was suspended outright — ⛔ **and the program now has NO in-flight guard on store health at all.** ⭐ **The Hub's own suggestion, unbuilt and unregistered: corroborate a depth dip with `n_live` before escalating** (a destroyed store should lose live items, not merely depth). *Where it lands:* C2W8/C2W10 monitor work; §7.27's watch item. Status: **`OPEN — a suspended rule with no replacement designed`** (§A23.1).
- ⭐⭐ **WOULD A STORE THAT ACTUALLY PAYS ITS WAY ERODE AT ALL? — the question C2W6's mechanism raises and cannot answer.** *What we haven't shown:* erosion behaviour on a store that is a **net benefit**. ⛔ **We have never had one:** the memory-deleted margin is **positive on 6/6 cell-seeds** (+0.105/+0.124/+0.226 bpc at w4; +0.0035/+0.0125/+0.0233 at w40) — *the block does better with the memory deleted.* *Why it matters:* ⭐ **the uselessness→erosion direction was demonstrated by ABLATING usefulness (residual OFF); the converse — restore usefulness, watch erosion vanish — has never been available to test**, so the mechanism is one-sided evidence. *Where it lands:* any wave that first produces a net-positive store (tier-iii); paper future-work. Status: **`OPEN — untestable until a store earns its bytes`** (**N225**).
- ⭐ **THE DEPTH PROTECTION'S BUDGET DEPENDENCE IS UNMAPPED — two points do not make a curve.** *What we haven't shown:* P1's depth effect anywhere except **w4 (2/3 seeds, geo 1.421×)** and **w40 (3/3, geo 3.628×)**. *Why it matters:* ⛔ **the shippable claim is w40-scoped**, and the two measured budgets disagree in strength and in seed-consistency; **the CSF3 config runs w40**, so the claim's only relevant point is the one with 3 seeds behind it. ⚠ **Seed variance dominates: untrained depth itself varies 5.4× across 3 seeds, and every SE is comparable to its mean.** *Where it lands:* the tier-iii ablation table; C2W8. Status: **`OPEN — a two-point "curve", declared`** (**N227**).
- ⭐ **DOES THE φ PLACEMENT LEAK HELP OR HARM? — we closed a channel without ever pricing it.** *What we haven't shown:* whether the 27 % of φ's layer-0 gradient that flows through the write is **useful signal** or **damage**. *Why it matters:* ⛔ **P1 closes it and bpc does not get worse at the 2-SE bar on either budget — that is the only evidence we have**, and it is a null over 1000 toy steps, not a statement about a 26–47 M run. ⚠ **And the leak is not layer-local: closing layer 0's path RAISES layer 1's φ gradient (0.0277 → 0.0435).** *Where it lands:* the run-3 ablation; the tier-iii write-up's mechanism paragraph. Status: **`OPEN — a channel closed on a no-harm null, not on a pricing`** (**N228**).
- ⭐ **I2 AT A RIG THAT COULD CARRY IT — and the rig change must be registered before it is run.** *What we haven't shown:* whether the most-useful wells erode fastest (or slowest). *Why it matters:* ⛔ **at 5–6 live wells/seed every ρ is a 6-point rank correlation, and the two usefulness proxies disagree in sign (−0.2571 ± 0.1512 vs +0.0667 ± 0.1627)**; ⛔⛔ **until it is answered, "depth is not quotable as feature importance" stays ACTIVE** (only the refutation branch could lift it). *The rig change named by the engineer:* **more live items (capacity/budget up, or longer sequences) or pooling across lanes — both change the rig and need registering first.** ⚠ **Floor-censoring is a real hazard the analyst pre-registered against: wells below 1e-30 are rank-degenerate.** *Where it lands:* **C2W10 (persistent store)**, per §A23.5. Status: **`TASKED (C2W10) — deferred by Head ruling; no rig change funded now`** (**N229**).
  - ⛔⛔ **AMENDED 2026-08-06 (fold-2, `c2w6-erosion-adjudication` §6) — THE PRICING CHANGED AND SO DID THE SHOPPING LIST.** ⛔ **Strike from the "why it matters" above: ~~the two usefulness proxies disagree in sign (−0.2571 ± 0.1512 vs +0.0667 ± 0.1627)~~ — `ρ(LOO) = +0.0667` is UNDEFINED, not a null** (ICC(1,1) **−0.205 / −0.218 / −0.248**, 3/3 seeds ⇒ attenuation ceiling **0.000**), so it was never a sign to disagree with. ✅ **Replacement ground: the two registered proxies are ANTI-correlated (−0.505 ± 0.125, 4.04 SE) and 8/18 wells are net-harmful.** ⭐ **AND the question is better posed than "underpowered": the current rig already EXCLUDES the Head's registered effect size (ρ_true ≥ +0.5 excluded at p ≈ 0.022 at the measured combined reliability 0.41); what it cannot do is separate ρ_true = 0 from |ρ_true| ≤ 0.4.** ⛔⛔ **THE BINDING CONSTRAINT IS THE RULE, NOT THE n — and this changes what C2W10 should buy.** Because *"confirm iff mean ρ ≥ +0.5"* thresholds **AT** the effect size, **P(confirm | true ρ = +0.5) = 0.424 at 6 wells × 3 seeds and only 0.468 at 100 × 3** ⇒ ⛔ ***buying wells against the current rule is money for nothing.*** ⭐ **Three things must be re-registered, IN THIS ORDER, before I2 re-runs at any scale:** **(i)** replace the threshold with a **TEST AGAINST 0** (Fisher-z CI, two-sided 5 %) — *free, and the binding constraint*; at 80 % power for true ρ_s = 0.5 that needs `seeds × (n_wells − 3) ≥ 26` at perfect reliability (**3 × 12 wells, or 6 wells × 9 seeds**) and **≥ 43** at the measured rel 0.648 (**3 × 17 wells, or 6 wells × 14 seeds**); **(ii)** an **ITEM-ID KEY** in the per-well telemetry — ⛔ **slot ≠ well** (same-slot site drift is **0.41 / 0.67 / 0.32×** the between-slot spread at place radius 0.30, so both "the well's erosion rate" and "the well's usefulness" are slot-level mixtures) — ⭐ *a prerequisite, not an upgrade*; **(iii)** a **LOO probe with positive ICC** (more batches/checkpoints) **or drop it and register a single primary proxy.** ⛔ **Only then is n_wells ≥ 17/seed worth the compute.** ⚠ **And put the budget at `w40`, not `w4`: it is where the science is and it is the least-powered cell** (5–6 wells, 17 readings, per-seed ρ spanning +0.872 … −0.771, and the only cell where erosion appears at all). ⛔⛔ **The depth-as-feature-importance caveat STAYS ACTIVE regardless.** Status: **`TASKED (C2W10) — UNCHANGED, but the rig change is now SPECIFIED and rule-first`** (**N229** fold-2 block / **N231**).
- ⭐ **`refresh_monotonic` (I1) HAS A BUILT GUARD AND NO HOME.** *What we haven't shown:* the invariant doing any work. *Why it matters:* **at w40 the channel is silent (64 events, 0 violations) and at w4 it fires at 22.8 % with the guard cleaning it to 0/165 post-guard** — ⇒ ⛔ **I1's value is a w4-budget artifact at this rig, and w4 is N94-demoted.** ⭐ **Its designed use-case is capacity pressure, repeated writes of the same item, and cross-stream persistence** — none of which exists in C2W6's rig. *Where it lands:* **C2W8 / C2W10**; it ships **OFF** in run 3 and costs nothing there. binding form already filed`**~~ ⇒ **Status: `→ SHOWN (C2W6 fold-2, 2026-08-06; c2w6-phi-leak-docstring + doc-curator-c2w6-fold-2)`** — ✅ **the precondition landed and the last mile RAN.** **Code side:** the engineer corrected **6 in-code sites** (comments/docstrings only, **zero behavioural change**; ### ⭐⭐ C2W6 FOLD-2 — the adjudication's boundaries (added 2026-08-06 by `doc-curator-c2w6-fold-2`, **[C2W6-ADJUDICATED]** / **[C2W6-CLOSE]**; charter **ADDENDUM 9 §A27–§A28**)

> **Context.** The analyst's re-spawn (`c2w6-erosion-adjudication`) re-derived **every** C2W6 number from raw and **found zero transcription or arithmetic error** (21 records × 5 curve scalars, worst relative deviation **0.00e+00**; all 12 cell-seed ρ triples MATCH). ⛔ **No verdict moved.** What it changed is **claim form, instrument validity and what the next rig must buy** — which is exactly this register's business. ⛔ **Everything remains TOY (0.16 M, 3 paired seeds); w4 is N94-demoted, w40 is not.** **Status moves folded above:** the **φ-qualifier last mile → `SHOWN`** · **I2 at a rig that could carry it → rule-first and specified** · **"does the non-reproduction survive scale?" → re-scoped to "does the RECOVERY survive scale?"**. **The tried-and-failed half is `negative_results.md` N231 + the fold-2 blocks on N223/N226/N227/N228/N229/N230.**

- ⭐⭐ **NET THE DESIGNED DECAY BEFORE ANY EROSION-RATE OR FLATTENING CLAIM — a build requirement, not a preference.** *What we haven't shown:* an erosion curve that separates **designed** decay from **learned** damage. *Why it matters:* C2W6's curve is **RAW**, and the designed decay's exponent **DRIFTS** — `last_write_chunk` moves **0→12 within one slot** across a run (6–10 distinct values per slot), so every reading carries a different factor `exp(−0.04·(15−c))`. ⭐ **The law itself is EXACT** (predicted per-tick relative depth drop **0.039211** vs measured median **0.039211** over **717** readings; own-leg residual ≤ **3.324e-07**), so this is a *netting* gap, not a correctness bug. **Netting moves E1 at seed 0 from 9.78× → 6.47× (−34 %)** — ⭐ **up to a third of the apparent "recovery" is ALLOCATOR DRIFT toward later write chunks, not depth restoration** — and it makes the arm look **MORE** eroded, never less. ⛔ **Direction is conservative and NO C2W6 verdict changes (E1 0/3 raw AND netted).** *Where it lands:* the **C2W8/C2W10 harness**, before any flattening claim; `aggregate()`'s curve path. Status: **`TASKED (C2W8/C2W10) — owner: engineer; Hub-dispositioned as a BUILD REQUIREMENT, explicitly NOT a C2W6 defect`** (**N226** fold-2 note / **N223** supersession block; A1).
- ⭐⭐ **AN EROSION GATE WHOSE REFERENCE READING SITS OUTSIDE THE TRANSIENT.** *What we haven't shown:* a decay statistic that is not config-coupled to its own denominator. *Why it matters:* ⛔⛔ **C2W6's registered E1 (`final ÷ step-200`) had its denominator INSIDE the trough on 2/3 seeds** — troughs at steps **150** and **275** — so it was structurally measuring **recovery-from-the-trough, not decay**, and that is mechanically why it read 9.78× and 0.53×. ⭐ **The transferable design rule: fix the reference OUTSIDE the transient** — `final ÷ max over the run`, or a **registered late-window baseline** — because *any fixed early step is coupled to a trough whose location moves with the config.* ⚠ **And the trigger shape for the in-flight monitor follows from the same fact: key on the TRANSIENT, not on a monotone trend.** *Where it lands:* the next registered erosion prereg (C2W8/C2W10); the replacement store-health detector. Status: **`OPEN — a specified estimator with no registered home yet`** (**N223** supersession block).
- ⭐⭐ **A USAGE-VS-EROSION RULE THAT CAN CONFIRM ITS OWN HYPOTHESIS — the registered one CANNOT, at any n.** *What we haven't shown:* an I2 decision rule with usable operating characteristics. *Why it matters:* ⛔⛔ **because *"confirm iff mean ρ ≥ +0.5"* thresholds AT the effect size, P(confirm | true ρ = exactly +0.5) is capped near 0.5 by construction — 0.424 at 6 wells × 3 seeds, 0.439 at 20×3, 0.468 at 100×3, 0.432 at 100×10** (and **0.282** at 6×3 at the measured reliability 0.648); symmetrically **P(false refute | true ρ = 0) = 0.123**. ⇒ ⛔ ***buying wells against the current rule is money for nothing*** — the rule, not the sample, is binding. ⭐ **The fix is free and must be registered FIRST: a test against 0 (Fisher-z CI, two-sided 5 %).** *Where it lands:* **C2W10's scoping** (a binding input per §A23.5); any future usage/importance measurement. Status: **`OPEN — a re-registration owed BEFORE any compute is spent`** (**N231**).
- ⭐ **AN ITEM-ID KEY IN THE PER-WELL TELEMETRY — slot is not identity.** *What we haven't shown:* any well-level quantity that is actually about a *well*. *Why it matters:* ⛔ **same-slot site displacement between consecutive readings is 0.443 / 1.525 / 0.533 against a between-slot spread of 1.084 / 2.278 / 1.668 — ratios 0.41 / 0.67 / 0.32 — at `atom_place_radius = 0.30`.** ⇒ **a slot's occupant changes materially over a run, so "the well's erosion rate" and "the well's usefulness" are both SLOT-LEVEL MIXTURES**, and every per-well correlation in C2W6 inherits that. ⭐ **The analyst's own framing: a prerequisite, not an upgrade.** *Where it lands:* the C2W8/C2W10 telemetry schema. Status: **`OPEN — a prerequisite for any well-level claim`** (**N231**).
- ⭐ **A USEFULNESS PROXY WITH POSITIVE RELIABILITY — we currently have one usable proxy, not two.** *What we haven't shown:* a validated well-level importance measure of any kind. *Why it matters:* ⛔⛔ **the leave-one-well-out proxy's ICC(1,1) is NEGATIVE on 3/3 seeds (−0.205 / −0.218 / −0.248) ⇒ reliability ≤ 0 and an attenuation ceiling of 0.000** — `|loo_delta_bpc|` median **1.15e-04** sits at the ~1e-4 cross-process float noise floor (measured with `--loo-batches 2`). ⚠ **The usable ones are read-selection (split-half Spearman-Brown 0.648) and erosion rate (0.633).** ⛔ **And the two registered proxies are ANTI-correlated (−0.505 ± 0.125, 4.04 SE), so they are not two views of one construct.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **there is NO validated well-level "importance" measure in this rig at all — which is the second, independent reason the depth-as-feature-importance caveat stays ACTIVE.** *The fix:* more LOO batches/checkpoints, **or** drop it and register a **single** primary proxy. *Where it lands:* C2W10. Status: **`OPEN — blocking on any well-level importance language`** (**N229** fold-2 block / **N231**).
- ⭐⭐ **WOULD A STORE WHOSE WELLS ARE *USED* RECOVER FROM THE TROUGH FASTER? — the mechanism question the trough newly opens.** *What we haven't shown:* what governs the **depth and duration** of the transient, or **why 1 of 3 seeds never dips at all.** *Why it matters:* the wave established *erosion tracks uselessness* on the **endpoint** comparison (residual-off geo **0.0775×**, −3.10 SE vs run-2 geo 0.327×, n.s.) — ⛔ **but the trough is a different object, and nobody has checked whether usefulness predicts trough depth, trough timing, or recovery rate rather than final depth.** ⚠ **The one thing we do know is negative: P1 does NOT prevent the transient** (`p1_on` min/untrained geo **0.105**, troughs 150/725/375), so **the surviving depth loss is not carried by the partitioned channel alone.** ⭐ **This is the cheapest live mechanism question in the fold — the curves are already banked.** *Where it lands:* a re-analysis of the C2W6 records (no new compute), then C2W8. Status: **`OPEN — answerable from banked artifacts, unowned`** (**N223** supersession block / **N225**).
- ⚠ **PRICE THE φ PLACEMENT LEAK AS A *DESIGNED* LEVER, NOT ONLY AS A CLOSED HOLE.** *What we haven't shown:* whether the write→φ channel **carried organization worth keeping.** *Why it matters:* ⭐ **the run-3 prereg (`PREREG-LeakAblation.md`) now registers the signal: a run-3 bpc regression beyond 2 SE ⇒ the write→φ channel carried organization ⇒ Add.9 §A28.1's DESIGNED write→φ lever re-prices UPWARD (urgent, not optional).** ⛔ **But the confound is declared in advance and is not funded here: the partition does TWO things at once (shield + leak closure), so a regression is attributable to the PAIR, and separating them needs §A28.1's designed arm.** ⚠ **Meanwhile the standing consequence is favourable and must travel with every banked ratio: every `gradient_probe` traj/point ratio at the run-1/2/3 config UNDERSTATES the read's share — ⭐ a CONSERVATIVE bias, never an inflation, so nothing is retracted and the ratios are LOWER BOUNDS.** *Where it lands:* CSF3 run 3; the tier-iii mechanism paragraph; §A28.1's designed arm. Status: **`TASKED-PARTIAL (run-3 registers the trigger; the SEPARATING arm is NOT funded)`** (**N228** fold-2 block; Add.9 §A27.3/§A28.1).
