**New boundaries this wave surfaced (eleven, schema-complete):**

- ⭐⭐ **ATTRACTOR–ANCHOR ALIGNMENT — the named next lever, unbuilt.** *What we haven't shown:* a write objective that places the store's **attractors at its designed anchors**. *Numbers:* the attractors sit **1.09 = ~1.4 `sep`** away, and the settle therefore scatters a committed allocation **4.834 ± 0.125 → 5.668 ± 0.396** distinct wells. *Why it matters:* ⭐⭐ **it is the Advisor's recommended first write-side lever precisely because its success criterion is ALREADY MEASURED — scatter 5.67 → ≈4.8, and the sign of `G1`** — and it is testable on the existing rig. *Where it lands:* the **write-side tier-ii iteration** (§A26.6(a)); the tier-ii write-up's mechanism paragraph. Status: **`OPEN — the highest-value unfunded item in this fold`** (**N233**; §A26.6).
- ⭐⭐ **A STORE WHOSE GEOMETRY IS QUERY-CONDITIONAL — or a family whose landscape is not written identically per query.** *What we haven't shown:* any vehicle where the settle **can** add query-specific information. *Why it matters:* ⛔⛔ **on this family every well is written on every query, so the launch launder upper-bounds the physics arm BY CONSTRUCTION** — no read design can escape that, and this is the fourth convergent datum on the same mechanism (C2W1 endpoint-trained landscape loses to a table · C2W2 trajectory write is a monotone cost · C2W4 streaming write digs no wells · C2W7 the organized store subtracts value from its own launches). *Where it lands:* §A26.6(b) — the family-level redesign, **if** attractor–anchor alignment moves scatter but not `G1`. Status: **`OPEN — a redesign, not a fix`** (**N232**; §A25.2).
- ⭐⭐ **`K6` HAS NEVER BEEN REPORTED PROSPECTIVELY.** *What we haven't shown:* the exactly-right fraction computed **before** a reader is fitted, on a cell that has not yet been scored. *Why it matters:* ⭐ **every use of it so far is retrospective** (18 % at C2W7, 2/2560 · 3/1280 · 0/2560 at C2W5) — and its whole value is that it is a **precondition**: it decides in advance whether a fitting artifact *can* move a number at all, with no store, no fit and no reader. *Where it lands:* the next reader registration of any kind (charter §A26.3/§A28.3 makes it mandatory there). Status: **`OPEN — registered as a standing precondition, never yet exercised in advance`** (**N237**).
- ⭐ **DOES A ZERO-PARAMETER MEMBER EVER CHANGE A *FUTURE* VERDICT?** *What we haven't shown:* a cell where the added zero-parameter reader is the member that decides an outcome. *Why it matters:* it is now **mandatory in every reader class** and it is genuinely free (0 params, 0 bytes, 3.7e-6 of the physics read) — but on the four cells measured it changed **nothing** (max gain **+0.0023**), and on three of them it was **strictly worse** (+0.00078 unseen; **+0.0109** on a real SEEN cell; **> 0.99** on a designed α-control). ⛔ **Which is exactly why it is ADDED, never substituted.** *Where it lands:* the reader-methodology appendix; every future tier-ii cell. Status: **`OPEN — a standing rule whose payoff is unmeasured after its founding case`** (**N237**).
- ⭐ **THE `sum_linear` INFLATION MAY BE A STORE FACT AND HAS NO OWNER.** *What we haven't shown:* whether `diag(W) = +46.53` at the cat-test cell is a reader pathology or a **store** pathology. *Numbers:* mean residual **13.48** against the identity's **1.84**; the settled payload block sits at ~**1/46** of the family's scale — **possibly the same basin-reach issue `tierii-read-fix` D10 measured.** *Why it matters:* ⛔ **it decides whether "a fitted reader's residual is never evidence a store is close" is an instrument caveat or a diagnosis of the physics arm.** *Where it lands:* an engineer with the physics arm in scope; the reader-methodology appendix. Status: **`OPEN — flagged by the engineer, owner needed`** (**N238**).
- ⭐ **A HEAD-TRAINING RECIPE THAT TRANSFERS.** *What we haven't shown:* any learned head that beats its own designed initialisation on unseen queries at the claim budget. *Numbers:* ⛔ **the designed/untrained head beats the trained one on every headline statistic** (best reader **0.0695 vs 0.0539**; `R1` **0.0883 vs 0.0656**; gated precision 0.571 vs 0.477; launch top-`F` 0.184 vs 0.103) — the training *did* improve SEEN (0.039 → 0.063) and **does not transfer**. *Why it matters:* ⭐ **this is w20's "free learning erases design" reproduced at the claim budget**, and **design-then-restrain remains the doctrine for every learned component entering tier ii** (§A25.3). *Where it lands:* the write-side iteration's head; any future learned launch component. Status: **`OPEN — the doctrine is confirmed; the recipe that would violate it does not exist`** (**N232**).
- ⭐ **ψ's MARGINAL VALUE AT A MULTIPLICITY READ — never measured, and structurally cannot be from this rig.** *What we haven't shown:* any ψ A/B at this read. ⛔ **The C2W7 vehicle contains no ψ at all** (grep-verified: 0 hits in `factored_store` / `multiwell_read` / `multiplicity_read` / `null_arms`), **so no hot-swap was possible.** *Why it matters:* ψ is the channel C2W5 measured as compressing 30–50 % of the delivered payload away (N222) — ⚠ **and its absence here is a SCOPE FACT, not an excuse: §A28.4(b) forbids offering it as the explanation.** *Where it lands:* CSF3 run-1/run-2 (tier iii), not this vehicle. Status: **`OPEN — declared NOT-RUN, structurally out of scope for this rig`**.
- ⚠ **A TWO-SIDED WELLS-VISITED GATE HAS NEVER GATED ANYTHING.** *What we haven't shown:* the re-registered instrument (`W/N_a`, two-sided labels) used **as a gate leg** on a real cell. *Why it matters:* the leg it replaces produced **one** attainable in-band value and mislabelled two opposite failures with the same word; the replacement is arithmetic, not measured, ⛔ **and its thresholds (what counts as under-usage? as concentration?) are UNSET.** *Where it lands:* the write-side iteration's prereg. Status: **`OPEN — re-registered, thresholds unspecified`** (**N239**; §A26.4).
- ⚠ **THE FULL 584-CONFIG RE-SCORE THROUGH ZERO-PARAMETER READERS.** *What we haven't shown:* identity columns on the **entire** `orgdiv-null-arms` grid. *Numbers:* only **N1 @ selected + the five argmax configs** were re-scored (plus tierii and cat-test physics arms); ⭐ **the trigger for the full grid was pre-registered — "iff something moves" — and nothing moved** (max gain +0.0023, 21× below the bar). *Why it matters:* the `null*` oracle bound rests on the grid, and the re-score's coverage of it is by-design partial. *Where it lands:* its own funded task **only if** a future finding re-opens it. Status: **`PARKED — its own registered trigger did not fire`** (**N237**).
- ⚠ **THE DEFERRED COMPONENTS, EACH WITH A DATED WAVE AND ITS OWN KILL-CONDITIONS (§A28.4(a), the anti-alibi discipline).** *What we haven't shown:* consolidation/trash (**C2W8**), traversal/wormholes (**C2W9**), a persistent store (**C2W10**). *Why it matters:* ⛔⛔ **"not built yet" always carries a date and a falsifier** — the intervention doc's Error 1 in mirror image — **and none of them may be offered as the explanation of C2W7's null.** *Where it lands:* the C2W8–C2W10 build waves as already scoped (Add.6 batch). Status: **`TASKED (C2W8 / C2W9 / C2W10) — dated, with kill-conditions owed at scoping`**.
- ⚠ **READ ITERATION 3.** *What we haven't shown:* nothing — **and that is the point.** ⛔⛔ **No third read iteration exists and none is scoped (§A26.6); registries, drafts and the ledger must not imply one is in flight.** *Why it matters:* the Advisor's §A25.2 reading is that **no read design can produce `G1 > 0` on this vehicle**, so a third iteration would be measuring the wrong side of the system. Status: **`PARKED — deliberately, by ruling`** (§A26.6).

**New boundaries this fold surfaces (six, schema-complete — none of them appear in the C2W5/C2W6/C2W7 blocks above):**

- ⭐⭐ **IS THE READER-CLASS PARAMETER CAP, RATHER THAN THE STORE, WHAT MAKES EVERY ARM READ 0?** *What we haven't shown:* any measurement that separates *"the store carries nothing"* from *"no reader under `< N_a·m = 256` parameters can extract what it carries."* *Numbers:* at `d = 8` the out-of-class combinatorial decoder reads **0.695** on the same launch information while every in-class reader reads **≈ 0** ⇒ **the information IS there and the gap is entirely decoder-side, inside a capped class.** *Why it matters:* ⛔ **three read protocols have now returned ≈ 0 and the cap has never been varied as an axis** — if the cap is binding, a family-level conclusion is being drawn from a reader-class constraint. ⚠ **The engineer raised exactly this in its §13.3 and no one owns it.** *Where it lands:* the write-side iteration's prereg (as a labelled diagnostic axis, never a claim cell — raising the cap is SP-1's out-of-class probe by definition); the tier-ii methodology appendix. Status: **`OPEN — raised by the report, unowned, and it would re-frame three waves of nulls`** (**N241/N242/N248**).
- ⭐ **THE OUT-OF-CLASS CEILING AS A STANDING PRE-FLIGHT INSTRUMENT, PER `(d, k, σ_q)`.** *What we haven't shown:* the ceiling computed **before** a cell is funded, as a routine gate rather than a post-hoc reconciliation. *Numbers:* it costs **~60 s and no store**, and it reads **0.072 / 0.272 / 0.695 / 0.982 / 0.992** across `(d, draws)` — ⭐ at `d = 4` it is **1.4× the arm bar**, i.e. it would have shown C2W5's cell was information-starved before a single store was written. *Why it matters:* it is the same family of instrument as `K0` and `K6` (**a precondition that tells you not to start**) and it is the cheapest of the three. *Where it lands:* the design-point checklist for any future compositional cell; the methodology appendix. Status: **`OPEN — proposed as doctrine by the report, never adopted`** (**N248(a)**).
- ⚠ **THE TRASH REGION'S DEPTH-THRESHOLD DECISION HAS NEVER FIRED.** *What we haven't shown:* any cell where the controller trashes a candidate **because it is too shallow**. *Numbers:* **476.4 candidates trashed for being over budget and 0.0 trashed below the depth threshold, at every seed** — the branch exists, is wired, and was never exercised. *Why it matters:* ⛔ **the lifecycle's *quality* criterion is therefore untested; only its *budget* criterion has run**, and C2W8 inherits it in that state. *Where it lands:* **C2W8** (it owns the lifecycle), as a named build check. Status: **`OPEN — inside a TASKED item, and easy to inherit as "tested"`** (**N246**).
- ⚠ **REACH AND SELECTIVITY HAVE NEVER BEEN BOUGHT TOGETHER.** *What we haven't shown:* any mechanism that raises coverage **without** costing gated precision, or vice versa. *Numbers:* learned `p₀` buys **12× coverage** (0.009 → 0.112) and **no** gated precision (0.350 vs 0.378); the descent gate buys **+0.048** gated precision (0.350 vs 0.302) and **costs** coverage (0.112 vs 0.152); `depth_ratio = 1` buys coverage **0.161** and lowers precision to 0.326. ⇒ ⭐ **every lever measured so far trades one for the other, and the gate discards correct wells along with wrong ones.** *Why it matters:* an exact-set metric needs **both**, so a trade-only lever set cannot clear it — this is the miniature form of the cardinality problem. *Where it lands:* the write-side iteration (if attractors sit at their anchors the trade may dissolve); the tier-ii mechanism paragraph. Status: **`OPEN — a pattern across four levers, never named as a constraint`** (**N245**).
- ⚠ **A HARNESS GUARD THAT DISTINGUISHES "FINISHED AND WROTE NOTHING" FROM "WAS KILLED".** *What we haven't shown:* any repo-level protection against the silent-OOM failure mode. *Numbers:* one full run lost at `[stage] arms` — **exit code 0, no traceback, no partial output**, ~**7 GB** taped by reverse-mode AD through a 300-step un-checkpointed `lax.scan` settle. *Why it matters:* ⛔ **the per-site fix is one decorator, but nothing prevents the next site** — and a return-code-checking harness reports the kill as a clean run. ⚠ **Sibling of the still-unbuilt `conftest.py` x64 guard (N72/N211), which is now on its fourth bill.** *Where it lands:* an engineering-debt task (small, unfunded); handover §7 (the Hub's document). Status: **`OPEN — remedy known per-site, no repo-level guard`** (**N249**).
- ⚠ **A PRE-FLIGHT ASSERTION ON `payload_radius / s`.** *What we haven't shown:* any automatic check that the launch can actually reach the well it is aimed at. *Numbers:* at `payload_radius / s = 2.8` the well's pull at the launch is **exp(−3.9) ≈ 2 %** of depth, the confinement wins, and settled occupancy precision collapses to **0.117 ≈ chance (0.125)** with distinct wells at 9.1; at 0.5 the same cell reads **0.253** and 11.7. *Why it matters:* ⭐ **the metric is scale-invariant in this radius, so it looks like a free knob and is not** — it is a basin-reach constraint, and a whole matrix can be run at a dead setting without anything printing a warning. ⚠ **The same wave separately lost its certificate band to a ruler measured at the wrong radius (`d/s` = 3.386 → re-measured 2.700–2.886).** *Where it lands:* the store-build path's design-time checks; the write-side iteration's prereg. Status: **`OPEN — measured twice, asserted nowhere`** (**N249** rider; deviation D10).

**New boundaries this fold surfaces (eleven, schema-complete — none of them appear in the blocks above):**

- ⭐⭐ **G-ADDR — AN ADDRESSABILITY LEG THAT CAN FAIL: does a read launched from `φ(x)` land in item x's OWN basin?** *What we haven't shown:* **any gate leg in this programme's history that measures this.** *Numbers:* both arms pass all three existing legs while reads land in no basin **58/62/62 of 64** (arm A) and `n_never_read = **16/16 on every seed**` (arm B), with launder margins **−0.354** and **−0.594** (**N262 / N263**). *Why it matters:* ⛔⛔ **it is the property the whole component exists to deliver, and the gate that licensed the pass-2 repair could not fail on it** — the third instance of *"a gate that cannot fail on the thing that matters"* in one wave. *Where it lands:* **the completed capture gate; the methodology appendix beside pass 1's forced-false conjuncts and C2W7's `S_eff` band.** Status: **`TASKED (C2W8 pass 3, §A30.1 — registered BEFORE anything else is scored: correct basin (not merely "a" basin), never-read fraction, launder margin; two-sided, designed negatives proving it CAN fail, pytest-asserted)`** ⛔ **and the arm race stays VOID until it exists.**
- ⭐⭐ **THE D2a COUNTERFACTUAL — is the near-zero drift an OUTCOME or an ALGEBRAIC IDENTITY?** *What we haven't shown:* that the attractor **can** move off the stored key when the write objective demands it. *Numbers:* median site drift **0.00100 / 0.00652 / 0.00150** against a key spacing of ~0.14 — a hundredfold margin, and **exactly the direction the pass-2 gate rewards**. *Why it matters:* ⛔⛔ **drift → 0 means the settled point approaches a deterministic function of the stored key = D2a = TABLE-EXPRESSIBLE, which intervention §8.2 prohibits — and the CIFAR arm has already measured settle = same-keys kNN to ±0.0007 at strong φ.** ⇒ **if the attractor cannot move, capture was bought by re-entering the forbidden configuration and the compliance ruling on `atom_site_local_init` REVERSES.** *Where it lands:* a perturbation/counterfactual cell attached to the mechanism. Status: **`TASKED (C2W8 pass 3, §A30.3 — a mechanical attached check, not an argument)`**.
- ⭐⭐ **AN OPERATING POINT WHERE A QUERY IS CLOSER TO ITS OWN KEY THAN TO ITS NEIGHBOUR'S (`σ_q < ` key spacing).** *What we haven't shown:* the census at any `(φ, σ_q)` where the items are separable at all. *Numbers:* `σ_q` **0.15** vs measured key spacing **0.1407 / 0.1375 / 0.1468**; repaired basin radii **2.0–2.9 σ_q** ⇒ **necessarily overlapping**; the kNN-in-φ launder itself decays **1.00 → 0.31** across the stream (**§A29.5**). *Why it matters:* ⛔ **every read-side number on this rig — ours and the launder's — is measured inside a regime where no read can succeed**, so the pass-2 addressability null is **attributable rather than confounded** only once this is fixed. ⭐ **It converges with the pass-1 CIFAR finding (0.161 → 0.319 by changing only the encoder): φ is the binding constraint on the CL rig.** *Where it lands:* the strong-φ census. Status: **`TASKED (C2W8 pass 3, §A30.2 — the wave's spine)`**.
- ⭐ **AN ANISOTROPIC ATOM SUPPORT — tight on the address axes, wide on the payload axis.** *What we haven't shown:* any store that decouples the reach the READ needs from the reach crowding forbids. *Numbers:* the selected support `R = 3.75` key spacings is set by the **payload** launch gap (`R > max|a_i| = 0.5`), **not** by address geometry, and foreign contribution rises monotonically with it (**1.3e-10 → 0.002 → 0.053 → 0.217** at width-fracs 0.5 / 1.0 / 1.5 / 2.0); at `frac ≤ 1.0` drift is **bimodal** — some wells at exactly 0.0 with `λ_min ≈ 42–50`, the rest at 0.45–1.18 with `λ_min = 0.1000 = 2α` **exactly** (the bare bowl, i.e. **no well at all at the relaxed point**). *Why it matters:* ⭐ **it is the trade-off both the pilot and the census-strength ablation bottom out on**, and it is the arm's own named next mechanism. ⚠ **The machinery already exists — `AtomDictionaryPotential.axis_width_scale` (w26) — and is NOT wired into `LearnedVStore`; the engineer declared it not built.** *Where it lands:* the write-side iteration (§A26.6). Status: **`OPEN — specified, machinery exists, unwired`**.
- ⭐⭐ **A SHARED WELL VOCABULARY THAT IS ACTUALLY TRAINED (the factored store).** *What we haven't shown:* **whether a shared vocabulary trains at all.** *Numbers:* the interface is **specified** (`head(φ_i, a_i) → w_i ∈ R^V`; store owns `{θ_v}`; item contribution `Σ_v w_iv · well(θ_v)`) and the shipped private case is asserted **BITWISE** as its degenerate `V = n_items`, one-hot special case (`compose_wells(*private_vocabulary(p)) == p` on all three parameter blocks) ⇒ ⭐ *"the arm does not foreclose the factored store"* **is a green test, not a promise**. *Why it matters:* ⛔ **the shipped arm is permanently claim-barred (`wells_per_item = 1`, `vocabulary_shared = false` ⇒ `NO_TIER_II_CLAIM`), so the tier-ii claim this component exists for cannot be made until a `V < n_items` vocabulary is trained.** *Where it lands:* the write-side tier-ii iteration. **New boundaries this fold surfaces (twelve, schema-complete — none of them appear in the blocks above):**

- ⭐⭐⭐ **WHY DOES THE SETTLE EXTRACT LESS FROM A CUE THAN 1-NN OVER THE SAME KEYS, WHEN THE GEOMETRY IS COMFORTABLE AND THE BASINS ARE REAL? — the wave's central open question, and the replacement for a retracted mechanism.** *What we haven't shown:* any mechanism for the addressability failure. *Numbers:* σ_q/spacing against the **real store population** is **0.32** (MNIST) / **0.19–0.37** (CIFAR) — comfortable — with **real basins** (46/48 at pass 2; A1 = 0.42–0.52) and **correct-basin ≈ 0.5**, and the settle **still** loses to 1-NN over the same keys (A3a −0.27…−0.44 on arm A; launder 0.79–0.90 vs store ≈0.5). *Why it matters:* ⛔⛔ **the previous answer was RETRACTED as wrong-population (N275), so the failure this whole component chain was built around currently has NO stated cause** — and ⭐ **it is consistent with the settle-destroys-launch-information family (N213, C2W7's scattering), which would make it a THEOREM-shaped result rather than a tuning problem.** *Where it lands:* a **small theorist task riding the next wave** (§A31.2 / §A32.5), the methodology chapter. Status: **`TASKED (theorist, rides C2W11 — priced as small, explicitly NOT blocking)`** ⛔ **theorist-priced, never assumed** (**N275**).
- ⭐⭐ **THE GATE HARDENING — four named repairs, and NO FUTURE CENSUS NUMBER IS QUOTABLE UNTIL THEY LAND.** *What we haven't shown:* a completed gate whose score is not a near-monotone function of settle-collapse. *Numbers:* **ρ(A1, G-DRIFT) = −0.967**, **ρ(A1, settle↔launder agreement) = +0.933** across 9 cells, while the per-arm flag `best_is_also_lowest_drift` reads **False on 3/3 arms, decided by 0.004 ties** (**N278**). *The four repairs, with owners assigned (§A32.3):* **(i)** a **two-sided drift leg or a drift FLOOR** (§A31.5) · **(ii)** **A1 reported as MARGIN-IN-SE**, not against a discrete threshold the best arm misses by one read (§A31.4/N280) · **(iii)** the **full-state co-scaling ruling** — the legal rescale includes the payload channel (§A31.6) · **(iv)** the **`covered` launch-point fix** (Hub-owned, N274). *Why it matters:* ⛔ **as written the gate rewards the degenerate configuration §8.2 prohibits.** *Where it lands:* **the `c2w8-close-gate-hardening` spoke, whose `GATE-HARDENING-DONE.json` GATES C2W11.** Status: **`TASKED (close-out spoke; ⛔ HAS RUN NOTHING at fold time — its results are OUT OF SCOPE here)`**.
- ⭐⭐ **THE POPULATION-CHOICE FIX — one defect that produced THREE separate consequences, none of them yet repaired in code.** *What we haven't shown:* any rig quantity normalised on the population it actually gates. *Numbers:* `median_nn_task1` is measured over **up to 200 task-1 keys** while the store holds **16 items** ⇒ **(a)** §A29.5's retracted root cause (≈1.07 quoted vs **0.32** measured) · **(b)** monitor #3's refusal rate **0.000 was ARITHMETIC** (`d_safe` sized on 200 keys, applied to 16 items; re-priced against the real population `d_safe`/spacing = **0.600–0.770** and refusals become **0.042 ± 0.021**) · **(c)** pass-3's **arm-dependent cue difficulty** (`cue_σ/codebook_spacing` **0.927 / 0.875 / 0.710**, a 30 % spread). *Why it matters:* ⭐ **these are ONE defect, not three, and fixing the population choice fixes all of them** — and until it is fixed, ⛔ **the `randconv`-vs-`pca` ordering is not safe to quote as a ranking** (N281). *Where it lands:* gate-hardening items (v) + (vi.6). Status: **`TASKED (gate hardening) — diagnosed three times, repaired zero times`**.
- ⭐⭐ **WHY IS THE FITTED ENCODER THE WORST ADDRESS? — the inversion is measured and UNATTRIBUTED, and one cheap cell decides it.** *What we haven't shown:* the mechanism behind `simclr − randconv` **A1 = −0.1406 ± 0.0508 (0/3 seeds)**. *Numbers / the two live candidates, both the engineer's:* **(i)** `simclr`'s codebook spacing **0.652** vs `randconv`'s **0.729** while the co-scaled atom width is *larger* (**0.856** vs 0.776) ⇒ **the compact supports may be over-overlapping**; **(ii)** the **l2-normalised contrastive φ concentrates the address cloud on a shell**, a different geometry from the one `atom_site_local_init` was tuned for. *Why it matters:* ⭐ **the design ruling that rides on this (address block ≠ task features, §A34.10) is currently supported by a MEASUREMENT WITHOUT A MECHANISM** — and if candidate (i) is right, the inversion is a **width-tuning artifact** rather than a property of contrastive features, which would materially change the ruling's scope. ⭐ **One width sweep at fixed φ decides it.** *Where it lands:* a small write-side cell; the design appendix. Status: **`OPEN — cheap, named by the spoke that measured the inversion, unfunded`**.
- ⭐⭐ **DOES THE NO-DAYLIGHT RESULT SURVIVE A NON-METRIC-NATIVE PROTOCOL? — the only question the tier-i null leaves open, and the entire reason C2W11 exists.** *What we haven't shown:* any read protocol where the store's answer is **not** expressible as a nearest-key lookup. *Numbers:* branch (b) **0/9 cells, 3/3 arms**, |A3b| ≤ 0.047 on 7/9 — the **third** substrate (gym → LM block → CL census) at which metric-native single-item recall shows no daylight (**N276**). *Why it matters:* ⛔⛔ **on a metric-native cue protocol 1-NN over the stored keys is the BAYES RULE, so this null is a theorem showing up, not a defeat — and no amount of physics can cross it there** (§A32.1 prohibits a pass 4). ⇒ ⭐ **the dividend can only structurally live at compositional/unseen queries (tier ii) or real streams (tier iii).** *Where it lands:* **C2W11's spine — the cat test on the repaired substrate** (§A32.4, §A33.2). Status: **`TASKED (C2W11) — the wave's whole premise`**.
- ⭐⭐ **THE THREE TIER-ii VALUE LEGS — registered, none built.** *What we haven't shown:* any VALUE measurement at all, at any tier (§A33.1: **VALUE legs exist ONLY at tier level with the tier's own control**). *The legs, ratified (§A33.2), all organizer-swapped:* **(i) generalization** — unseen-combination performance through the k-particle read + ψ · **(ii) calibration** — the **graded-novelty** channel scores known/novel features correctly (*"close to dog, not a dog, snout anomalous"*; per-particle capture/depth/residual composed per feature + overlap-as-confidence, trainable via feature-dropout-as-pseudo-novelty; ⭐ **flagged by the Head as a major novelty piece**) · **(iii) navigability** — answer quality vs **read compute** (⭐ **the anytime curve returns, now as the navigation-cost metric**). *Why it matters:* ⛔ **the whole C2W8 arc produced ZERO value evidence by design, and the refined question is not only *"did the physics build semantic structure"* but *"was that structure easy enough to NAVIGATE to make an educated guess on an unseen input, and with what confidence"*.** *Where it lands:* **C2W11**. Status: **`TASKED (C2W11, §A33.2) — specified, unbuilt`**.
- ⭐ **A LABEL-FREE ORGANIZER WITH SUPERVISION AT THE READ HEAD — ruled, never built, and the pass-3 inversion is its evidence base.** *What we haven't shown:* any self-supervised organization objective training φ/placement toward semantic metric structure through the write. *Numbers/basis:* **task supervision built the WORST address geometry beyond 2 SE** (N277) ⇒ **a purely supervised organizer is the MEASURED wrong bet**, and a task-specific organizing loss would also fragment the store (the Head's foundation-model objection). *Shape, ratified (§A34.6):* **self-supervised organization** (co-occurrence / reconstruction-through-ψ / prediction) **+ a supervised downstream head for the benchmark** **+ an optional weakly-supervised organizer arm raced as an ablation**; downstream heads swappable, **the store is the foundation object**. *Why it matters:* it is the first time this program's training regime has been ruled on **measured** rather than assumed grounds. *Where it lands:* **C2W11 (§A34.6/§A34.9(a))**. Status: **`TASKED (C2W11) — ruled, unbuilt`**.
- ⭐ **KINETICS AS WITHIN-WELL SELECTORS — the mechanism REQUIRES a channel that does not yet exist in the shipped read.** *What we haven't shown:* any read whose kinetics matter. *Numbers/theory (ratified §A34.5):* dissipation is a **drag force `−γp`**, so `v̇ = −(1/m)∇V − γv` and **mass sets the damping ratio** (ζ ∝ √m at fixed γ, λ) — light = underdamped, heavy = overdamped; **mass-blindness holds ONLY for the fully-settled endpoint in a strictly convex well** (Prop F1 unchanged). *Levers in ratified order:* **friction first** (the measured 14× channel; spatial `γ_φ(q)` × per-particle multipliers, **both already plumbed**) · **mass second** · ⭐ **spectral/per-direction mass** (per-address mass, shipped C2W7) as the richer selector — `M⁻¹∇V` with a mass **tensor** tilts the roll direction so two particles from one launch reach different sub-wells. ⛔ **The mechanism REQUIRES any of: within-well flat/soft floors · finite-budget reads · trajectory reads — and T3 is why: settled-point kinetic gradients are BITWISE ZERO.** *Why it matters:* ⛔ **kinetics heads can only be trained through those channels; wiring them into a settled-point read trains nothing.** **Nomenclature (Def-2): this item is about INERTIAL `M` (and its spectral/per-direction form), ⛔ never spectral μ.** *Where it lands:* **C2W11 priced ablations (§A34.5/§A34.9(f))**. Status: **`THEORY-ONLY → TASKED (C2W11) as a priced ablation, ⛔ not all-at-once (anti-collapse §3.1)`**.
- ⭐ **HIERARCHICAL PLACEMENT MAINTAINED BY THE OBJECTIVE — specified, and the w20 guard says the obvious implementation fails.** *What we haven't shown:* any two-scale geometry (wide/shallow coarse basins + narrow/deep feature wells) that **survives training**. *Basis (§A34.2):* it rides the existing two-phase settle (`γ_address` = coarse, `γ_read` = fine), and ⛔ **the w20 guard is binding: the hierarchy must live in the WRITE OBJECTIVE, never only in init** (this program has repeatedly measured designed structure surviving in init and dying under learning). ⛔ **Wells are never named semantically (§A4.5).** *Why it matters:* it is the structural precondition for feature-factored launches meaning anything. *Where it lands:* **C2W11 (§A34.2)**. Status: **`OPEN → TASKED (C2W11) — specified, unbuilt, with its own known failure mode`**.
- ⚠ **`randconv`'s ONE-READ STRADDLE — a seed-count question that was declared, not run.** *What we haven't shown:* whether the best arm's A1 is genuinely at 0.237 or straddling the 0.25 threshold. *Numbers:* **31 / 31 / 29 of 128** against a threshold of **32/128** — it fails **by ONE read** on 2 of 3 seeds; and a **legal, scale-covariant rescale (`a = 0.8`) moves A1 by +0.0469 — INSIDE the registered 0.05 guard — and FLIPS the verdict False → True** (**N280**). *Why it matters:* ⛔ **a verdict that turns on one read out of 128 is not a verdict**, and it is the concrete instance behind the *"report margin-in-SE"* hardening item. ⭐ **5–7 seeds would settle it; the engineer declared it explicitly as a SEED-COUNT question and NOT a tuning question.** *Where it lands:* the hardened gate's first re-score. Status: **`OPEN — declared NOT-RUN by the spoke that found it`**.
- ⚠ **`pca_whiten` — implemented, declared SECONDARY, never run; and §4 warns it is exactly the class of move that can look like information gain.** *What we haven't shown:* whether equalising per-axis variance moves σ_q/spacing further than the fitted PCA map. *Numbers:* the shipped map forms measured **`pca` 0.248/0.210/0.186**, `truncate` **0.241/0.213/0.186** (⭐ **numerically ≈ the fitted projection AT THIS ENCODER, because `simclr`'s read-in already ends in a PCA head — the defect the wave fixed is one of PRINCIPLE and LEDGER, not a large numerical loss**), `gaussian` 0.248/0.235/0.210. *Why it matters:* ⚠ **an axis rescale is precisely the move the anti-trivial-pass guard exists to catch** — so it must be run **with** that caveat or deleted. *Where it lands:* the φ/geometry instrument. Status: **`OPEN — the spoke's own recommendation: run it with the caveat, or delete it`**.
- ⚠ **A CENSUS THAT REFUSES TO RUN AT A NON-SELECTED WIDTH — the trap is still live for every OTHER caller.** *What we haven't shown:* any guard preventing a caller from silently scoring the wrong store. *Numbers:* `ExperimentCaptureArmAConfig.atom_width_frac_spacing` defaults to **0.5** (the pilot cell) while the **banked** census ran at **1.5**, and at the default the arm **does not clear the pass-2 gate** (G-DEC exactly at chance 3/3, G-DRIFT 0.61–0.71, depth 0.20 vs 0.61). ⭐ **The spine did NOT inherit the trap** — it declares 1.5 in its own config group and **pins it with a test** — ⛔ **but the underlying default is unchanged and any other caller of `exp_capture_armA` silently scores the pilot store.** *Why it matters:* ⛔ **it is a silent-wrong-answer hazard, not a bug: everything runs and the numbers look plausible.** *Where it lands:* the census's own config (gate-hardening adjacent). Status: **`OPEN — Hub-owned; either change the default or make the census REFUSE at a non-selected width`**.

**New boundaries this fold surfaces (schema-complete — none appear in the blocks above):**

- ⭐⭐⭐ **THE ATOM-WIDTH RE-SELECTION — a banked, measurement-chosen setting that silently stopped meaning what it meant.** *What we haven't shown:* any width selected against the population the store actually holds. *Numbers:* every arm store-config factory recovers the spacing as **`d_safe / d_safe_frac`**, so repairing `d_safe`'s population moved the co-scaled width onto a spacing **~3× larger (0.445 vs 0.141, MNIST**; ratios **1.567 / 1.435 / 1.843**) ⇒ ⛔ **`atom_width_frac_spacing = 1.5` is NOT inherited**, and the banked spine's achieved widths (0.621–0.856) belong to the old population. *Why it matters:* ⛔⛔ **until a new selection exists, NO post-repair census number is comparable to a banked one** — and the failure mode is silent (everything runs, the numbers look plausible). *Where it lands:* **C2W11 spoke A must RE-SELECT and DECLARE it**, enforced by the refuse-at-unselected-width guard (`PREREG-C2W11.md` §2.1(b)). Status: **`TASKED (C2W11 spoke A) — mandatory, and the guard makes drift impossible rather than merely discouraged`** (**N290**).
- ⭐⭐ **THE DRIFT FLOOR'S VALUE — registered, anchored to one measured point, and it already fails a banked cell.** *What we haven't shown:* that `0.01 × codebook spacing` is the right floor rather than a defensible one. *Numbers:* the floor sits **2×** above a banked adjudicated table-expressible store (~**0.005×** spacing); the planted negative reads **0.0042**; ⛔ **the banked pass-3 arm-A seed-0 cell reads 0.0071 and would FAIL.** *Why it matters:* ⭐ **the repaired census is STRICTLY HARDER than the one every banked C2W8 number was measured on**, so a C2W11 cell failing there is **not** a physics regression — but a floor that is too high fails healthy stores and a floor that is too low restores the D2a reward. *Where it lands:* ⚖ **a Hub confirmation BEFORE C2W11 scores anything** (the spoke's own reconciliation item 6). Status: **`OPEN — one Hub decision, explicitly requested by the spoke, not yet taken`** (**N291**).
- ⭐ **THE BANKED C2W8 CELLS RE-SCORED UNDER THE REPAIRED CENSUS — bought by the repair, funded by nobody.** *What we haven't shown:* what any C2W8 arm scores on the instrument we now ship. *Numbers:* ⛔ **zero arms were re-run** (arm A, arm B, the spine's 9 cells); the only post-repair statements about banked cells are **recomputed from banked telemetry** (the 0.0071 drift; arm B's settle-side A1 0.9297/0.9844/0.8750). *Why it matters:* ⚠ **every cross-instrument comparison in the program now needs the instrument named, and the cheapest way to retire that caveat is one re-score** — ⛔ **but a re-score is a measurement, and nobody has priced it.** *Where it lands:* whoever owns the census after C2W11's spoke A. Status: **`OPEN — unfunded; and until it exists, "with the instrument named" is mandatory on every C2W8 number`**.
- ⭐⭐ **K6 — THE PRECONDITION THAT WAS BANKED SIX TIMES AND IS NOW OWNED.** *What we haven't shown:* the exactly-right fraction on any C2W11 family. *Numbers/standing form:* ***"a fitted reader destroys signal only in proportion to the fraction of queries whose asserted set is already exactly right"*** — **C2W5: 2/2560 · 3/1280 · 0/2560** (so the pathology cost C2W5 nothing); **C2W7: ~18 %** (so it cost C2W7 real signal). ⭐ **Computable in one line BEFORE any reader is fitted, with no store.** *Why it matters:* ⛔ **a fitted-reader `0` is quotable ONLY beside the zero-parameter member's score on the same latent** — this fraction is what makes the difference interpretable. *Where it lands:* **`PREREG-C2W11.md` §4 — spoke A (`c2w11-substrate-and-kills`), a MANDATORY REPORTED precondition (MECHANICS, not a kill).** Status: **`TASKED (C2W11 spoke A) — the reporting duty is CLOSED after six bankings`** ⚠⚠ **but the CITATION is not: the prereg's K6 row and this fold's task file both cite `§A28.3`, which N286 records as a typo for §A26.3 (correction owner = the ADVISOR). ⛔ Preregs and task files are never edited — flagged, not resolved.**
- ⭐⭐ **THE ψ-DOES-THE-WORK FALSE POSITIVE — the wave's most likely way to produce a wrong headline, and it now has a number.** *What we haven't shown:* that a tier-ii clear would be the store's doing rather than the readout's. *Numbers (registered priors, `PREREG-C2W11.md` §8 Q11):* **P(ψ does the work | V1 clears) = 0.15 with BOTH the measured guard (K4 at full ψ) AND the K8 structural cell · 0.40 on the measured guard alone.** ⭐ **The structural argument: at `K < N_a` the `1_A ↦ y` design matrix is rank-deficient, so the SP-1 linear-code probe PROVABLY cannot recover the payloads** — verified at C2W5's `K = 12 < N_a = 16` fixture. *Why it matters:* ⛔⛔ **a false positive here IS the tier-ii headline**, and ⭐ **the 0.15-vs-0.40 gap is itself the argument for buying the structural cell.** *Where it lands:* **C2W11, one confirmatory cell at the headline configuration only.** Status: **`TASKED (C2W11) — registered in advance so a passing K4 MOVES a number rather than merely reassuring`**.
- ⭐ **M8 — THE CURVATURE-SPECTRUM CHECK, because term (c) has no other consumer this wave.** *What we haven't shown:* that a designed within-well **soft direction** survives superposition and training. *Numbers/banked traps:* §A4.2 **REFUTED** the *tilt* instantiation on a learned store (tilt monotonically reduces `λ_min`, **+0.099 → −8.28**); the shipped confinement floors the soft mode at **`2α = 0.1000`**, and ⚠ **undug wells report `λ_min ≈ 0.0993` BECAUSE `2α` is what `λ_min` reports when nothing was written** — so *"a soft direction"* means a λ **at or near the floor that is not the floor itself**. *Why it matters:* ⛔ **w20's law says undefended designed structure gets ERASED, and without M8 an undefended term (c) could be silently tuned to inertness with nobody knowing** — the §A4.2 lesson applied **prospectively** rather than after the fact. Hub prior **Q10 = 0.25**. *Where it lands:* **C2W11 (§A34.9(c) + Amendment 1's M8)**. Status: **`TASKED (C2W11) — registered, unbuilt; and it is the term's ONLY consumer this wave`**.

**New boundaries this fold surfaces (schema-complete — none appears in the blocks above):**

- ⭐⭐⭐ **WHAT A PERSISTENT-STORE *VALUE* VENUE CAN BE, NOW THAT THE STREAMING-PREDICTION TRACK IS CLOSED — and the answer moves to COMPOSITIONAL territory.** *What we haven't shown:* any venue where a persistent store can be worth something and **a table does not win by theorem**. *Numbers that closed the old route:* criterion 4 has **six** confirmations, the last two measured here — INSECTS `b2_margin_pts = −1.8983` (exemplar 76.9157 % at 0.634 MiB vs ARF-100 78.8139 %) and Metro `m2_margin_rel = −0.0615` (exemplar **MAE 314.575** at 0.634 MiB vs tuned GBDT **335.203** at 3.6 MB), the latter firing against **all nine** strong references. *Why it matters:* ⭐⭐ **this is a fallback being correctly retired, not a crisis** (§A41.1) — but the question *"does a persistent store help?"* is now **unhomed**, and the wave's own hand-forward names the shape of the answer: ***"does a persistent store help across compositional streams"*, on a venue where a table does not win by theorem**, **if C2W11's tier-ii claim lands.** ⭐ **The lifecycle ships ready for that rig** (usage-gated promotion with hysteresis · demotion to designed decay, never to trash · `read_hits`-keyed trash · bounded protected fraction · netting on every curve). *Where it lands:* **C2W11's outcome, then the next VALUE scoping; the Head reserves the venue question.** Status: **`OPEN — recorded as a DESIGN INPUT, not a leftover (the C2W5→C2W7 pattern)`** (**N294 / N295**; Add.14 closing paragraph).
- ⭐⭐ **THE FOUR-TEST VENUE BATTERY — priced at ≈1 h of CPU, offered, and funded by nobody.** *What we haven't shown:* any candidate venue screened on all four of the criteria this wave discovered it needs. *The battery, as the analyst filed it:* **(1)** strong baselines present · **(2)** headroom measured against **the strongest TRIVIAL rule, not against persistence** (on Metro: 41.6 % vs persistence = pass, **2.17 % vs the weekly naive = fail** — both true, only the second informative) · **(3)** the **regime variable OUTSIDE the query** · **(4)** **shuffle-sensitivity at the TARGET byte budget** (on Metro the fixed-seed pair-shuffle *improved* the byte-matched store, **320.982 → 311.748**). *Why it matters:* ⛔ **the wave paid for two venue builds' worth of scoping to learn each of these separately, and any future venue proposal that skips them is buying the same lesson a seventh time.** *Where it lands:* the next VALUE-venue scoping; the intervention doc's §6 criteria if the Advisor adopts it. Status: **`OPEN — specified, cheap, unfunded; the criterion-2 re-registration ("headroom against the best trivial predictor available, printed in every band table") is the half that needs an Advisor ruling`**.
- ⭐⭐ **THE BYTE-FRONTIER SLOPE RULE — a two-gate observation that predicts what a THIRD venue must look like, and it is SPOKE-PROPOSED, not adopted.** *What we haven't shown:* that the rule holds anywhere except the two venues that produced it. *Numbers:* INSECTS — the regime variable (temperature) is **withheld from the 33 features**, and exemplar accuracy **DECREASES** with store size, **76.03 % @ L=500 → 59.75 % @ L=14,782**; Metro — the clock is **fully recoverable from each pair's own 24-lag window**, and MAE **decreases monotonically**, **407.91 @ L=250 → 304.02 @ L=34,847, with no interior optimum.** *Proposed form:* ***the sign of an exemplar store's byte-frontier slope is set by whether the regime variable sits inside or outside the query*** ⇒ corollary, a design rule for the next venue: **the regime variable must be OUTSIDE the query AND the store's byte-frontier must be non-degenerate.** *Why it matters:* ⭐ it is the cheapest publishable-looking product of two admissibility gates (~5 h CPU total) **and** the only thing in the wave that tells a future scoper what to look for. ⚠⚠ **Flagged, not adopted: the Hub's close entry and Add.14 do NOT carry this as a ruled finding — it is the metro spoke's §3.3/recommendation 2, transcribed here so it is not lost, and it needs a Hub/Advisor decision before it appears in any paper-facing artifact.** *Where it lands:* the B′ protocol-evidence write-up, if ratified. Status: **`THEORY-ONLY (n = 2 venues) — spoke-proposed, ⛔ NOT Hub-adopted`**.
- ⭐⭐ **THE SHUFFLE NULL AND THE LABEL-EMBARGO TABLE AS STANDING PRACTICE — both proposed at close, neither adopted.** *What we haven't shown:* either check applied to a venue **before** its build, other than retroactively on Metro. *Numbers:* the shuffle costs **20 s of CPU** and killed Metro on its own (order worth **≤ 0** at the wave's budgets, **+4.71 % only below ~2,000 exemplars**); the embargo is **three lines** and caught a **+10.85 %** artifact inside a pre-registered protocol, **asymmetric in the direction of firing** (k-NN L=250 std 398.17 → 446.64; GBDT 339.80 → **338.65 = −0.34 %**). *Why it matters:* ⛔ **a venue where a fixed-seed pair-shuffle does not degrade the byte-matched store CANNOT host a retention claim, whatever the tripwire says** — that is a *third*, independent kill criterion the program did not have before this wave. *Where it lands:* every future VALUE-venue gate; the benchmarking-methodology write-up (**N296**). Status: **`OPEN — PROPOSED standing practice ×2, ⛔ not adopted; the methodology finding itself is banked`**.
- ⭐⭐ **RELIABILITY-PLUS-MAGNITUDE AS A STANDING INSTRUMENT RULE, AND ITS float64 CONSEQUENCE.** *What we haven't shown:* any undesigned-erosion measurement made on an instrument whose noise floor is **below** the effect it hunts. *Numbers:* `E_netted` split-half **0.885 / 0.839 / 0.778** while the quantity is **≈4 float32 ULP** — netted total log-drop **4e-7 nats** against **0.58–0.62 nats** raw, ratio **6.8e-7**; ⇒ **at float32 the netted channel saturates and the instrument's noise floor sits ABOVE any effect it could see.** *Proposed rule:* every erosion/usage proxy carries **(a) reliability AND (b) magnitude against the designed channel**; **≤1e-6 of the designed channel ⇒ UNDEFINED regardless of reliability.** *Why it matters:* ⛔ **without it, a highly reliable measurement of round-off reads as a result** — the exact false positive the spoke's own refuted prereg (P-4) would have licensed. *Where it lands:* **Advisor (the twin of C2W6's `ICC ≤ 0 ⇒ UNDEFINED`)**; the next undesigned-erosion cell, which must run **float64 store amplitudes**. Status: **`OPEN — PROPOSED (Hub → Advisor), ⛔ not adopted; the float64 requirement is the concrete half and has no owner`** (**N298**).
- ⭐ **DOES PROTECTION EVER *STICK*? — the protected set never accumulated, and the parameter that would fix it is unregistered.** *What we haven't shown:* any run in which the protected population approaches its bound. *Numbers:* `protected_cap = floor(0.25 × 64) = 16`, measured peak **3 / 1 / 3** wells, `n_promote_refused = 0` on every seed ⇒ **L4 UNEXERCISED (K-C: not working and not broken)**; the cause is measured — **60 promotions vs 53 demotions, churn ≈ 1:1**, with items promoting and demoting 1–2 chunks apart, because **usage is bursty on a 2-chunk timescale** while `d_demote = 2`. *Why it matters:* ⛔ **a protected-fraction bound that is never approached has never been tested by a stream**, and the fix is a **registered parameter change (`d_demote` > the usage burst gap), not a code change** — i.e. cheap, but a Hub decision. *Where it lands:* whichever rig next runs the lifecycle (the compositional one, if C2W11 lands). Status: **`OPEN — a parameter re-registration, explicitly declared a Hub decision and not a spoke's`** (**N301**).
- ⭐ **THE TRASH CRITERION'S TWO READINGS — both ship, and nobody has ratified which one §A20.6 means.** *What we haven't shown:* which population *"never useful over `k` stream boundaries"* is supposed to name. *Numbers/fact:* the registered wording (*"since first appearance"*) and the registered designed negative (*"useful in stream 1 only ⇒ trashed at `k`"*) are **not jointly satisfiable**; `last_k_streams` (**the shipped default**) satisfies all three negatives, `since_first_seen` is the literal wording under which negative (a) does **not** fire, and **the difference is pytest-asserted so it cannot be silently resolved.** *Why it matters:* ⛔ **it changes WHICH wells are trashed — not whether the verb works** — and a later silent choice would be invisible in every downstream number. *Where it lands:* **Hub/Advisor ratification of §A20.6's intent.** Status: **`OPEN — one ratification, requested by the spoke, not yet given`** (**N313** ⚠ *[dated pointer repair 2026-08-13, `doc-curator-c2w11-claims-matrix-fold` Part C: cited **N302** = the C2W10 spec-defects entry (✯), renumbered `N302 ✯ → N313` by Hub ruling 2026-08-13 to clear the collision with the C2W11 fold's `N302 ✰`; **⛔ pointer only — the item's content, status and ownership are unchanged.** See **N315**]*).
- ⭐⭐ **THE LAUNCH-POINT COVERAGE CEILING — 45–51 % of reads are credited to nobody, and every usage number inherits it.** *What we haven't shown:* a usage proxy measured at the **settle** side of the read. *Numbers:* `read_coverage` **0.464 / 0.452 / 0.514**; **3225 / 3297 / 2926 of 6016** read events unassigned **by design**; **20 / 44 / 24 never-read wells** per seed create large `U = 0` tie blocks ⇒ effective power **below** nominal n, on top of the 0.59–0.73 attenuation ceiling. *Why it matters:* ⛔⛔ **part of the trashed population is *"never reachable by this read"*, not *"never useful"*** — the trash verb is exercised (117 routings), but its target set is not a pure never-useful set at this coverage; and every `ρ` computed on `read_hits` inherits the attenuation. ⭐ **This is the C2W8 `launch_covered` vs `settle_covered` split arriving in the lifecycle's usage proxy — the same defect class, one layer up.** *Where it lands:* the usage telemetry (settle-side crediting), before any further trash-criterion claim. Status: **`OPEN — diagnosed and quantified; read_coverage travels with every usage number as an interim measure`** (**N301** / **N297**).
- ⭐ **THE REAL-STREAM LIFECYCLE LEGS HAVE NEVER CONSUMED A REAL STREAM.** *What we haven't shown:* the loader, sha256 reproduction gate, decimation ladder and structure assertions running on anything but the synthetic regime-switcher. *Numbers/fact:* the machinery **ships and is pytest-pinned** (digest recomputed with raise-on-mismatch; a missing gate file handled as a **NOT-RUN**, not a crash; decimation refuses an `m` off the registered ladder; structure preservation asserted **with counts**) — but `BENCHMARK-GATE.json` did not exist when the build ran, and both venues were subsequently ruled inadmissible, so ⛔ **the real-stream path is a DECLARED NOT-RUN with no venue to run on.** *Why it matters:* ⚠ **untested-on-real-data plumbing is exactly the kind of asset that is assumed working when a future wave finally has a venue** — and the reproduction gate is *"mismatch = hard stop"*, so a first real use will be loud rather than silent. *Where it lands:* the compositional rig, or whatever venue survives the four-test battery. Status: **`OPEN — built, green on synthetic, never fed a real stream`** (**N301**).
- ⚠ **THE DEPTH ENDOGENEITY OF `U` — a one-line check that would close a live watch item.** *What we haven't shown:* that a `read_hits`-keyed trash criterion is independent of depth. *Numbers:* `ρ(U, depth_raw) ≈ +0.182 / +0.224 / +0.190` (⛔ **below the 0.272/0.265/0.272 detection floor and age-confounded — never quotable as positive evidence**), `ICC(U)` 0.28–0.47. *Why it matters:* ⭐ **it is a watch item, not a defect** — but if `U` tracks depth, a `read_hits` criterion is partly a depth criterion, which is the one thing §A28.3(ii) forbids the trash verb from being. *The fix, named by the spoke:* add `ρ(read_hits, depth_raw)` per seed to the census output so **L3's criterion carries its own endogeneity number.** *Where it lands:* the lifecycle harness's census emission. Status: **`OPEN — one line, unowned`** (**N301**).

## CAFE / C-MAPSS evaluation protocol & the encoder-swap claim (waves 17–18 fold, added 2026-07-23 by `ledger-w17-w18-catchup`)

> **Context.** Waves 17–18 put CLU on a public benchmark for the first time and discovered that *the protocol*, not the number, is the open question. **All items below are live** — none is superseded by w19/w20 (which re-gated the encoder task but did not answer any of these). ⚠ **Standing scope guard:** every FD00x number quoted here is **PROVISIONAL pending the `dt` fix** (N63), and **no CAFE C-MAPSS number is externally comparable** (the label bug).

- **⭐⭐ The matched-head (protocol-3) encoder swap — CLU has NEVER been evaluated under the protocol its comparator uses.** *What we haven't shown:* CLU as a frozen encoder feeding HEPA's **198K dt-conditioned MLP head** (`[h_t;Δt]: 257→256→256→256`, GELU, **raw unnormalized Δt**, + shared `LayerNorm+Linear→1`; 197,632 + 769 = **198,401**), positive-weighted BCE, identical labels/splits. *Why it matters:* every CLU number to date is **protocol 4** (CAFE `encode()` → default CoxPH); the paper's encoder-isolated number is **protocol 3** (0.73 ± .02 on C-MAPSS-1) and its full-system number is **protocol 2** (0.81 ± .03) — **the head alone is worth ≈0.08 h-AUROC**, so **CLU's 0.7168 has no apples-to-apples comparator at all**. Under a swap we never claim a number against HEPA-the-system, only an **encoder-for-encoder delta under one fixed protocol** — the Head's framing, and far more portable ("CLU as a drop-in encoder block") than "CLU beats HEPA on turbofans". **App. G publishes the peer column for free** (Chronos-2 / MOMENT / TFM-2.5 / Moirai under the same head), and **App. H is the published precedent for the design** (*"only the SSL objective differs"*). *Where it lands:* the `clu-horizon-encoder` task (**still UNWRITTEN**; re-gated at w19 on the read-in/addressing question) / ICLR. Status: `OPEN` — **the spec is in hand** (Head-supplied `HEPA_predictor.py` is ground truth over any reconstruction).
- **The per-horizon "graceful decay" figure is only live under protocol 3.** *What we haven't shown:* that CLU's degradation over horizon is model-discriminating at all. *Why it matters:* under CAFE's default CoxPH probe a risk score induces a **bit-identical sample ranking at all 125 horizons** (rank corr **1.0000**, identical permutation) — per-horizon AUROC varies **only because the labels change** — so the scout's proposed headline figure is **dead under the default probe** and **live only under the dt-conditioned matched head**. ⇒ **this is a scientific reason to adopt the matched head, not merely a comparability argument.** Status: `OPEN` (rides with the entry above).
- **⭐ "HEPA-extrapolated" (`Δt > K`) — the correct rollout experiment, and a one-liner.** *What we haven't shown:* how a horizon-conditioned predictor behaves **beyond its training horizon**, against CLU's iterated dynamics. *Why it matters:* **HEPA ships NO multi-step path and NO beyond-horizon evaluation ⇒ we are not preempted**; the predictor accepts any scalar Δt (confirmed in the Head's source). ⛔ **The "HEPA-iterated" arm is a STRAW MAN** — `ĥ` is trained to regress a **bidirectional, attention-pooled read of the FUTURE** while `h_t` is a **causal read of the PAST**, so feeding `ĥ` back inputs a distribution the predictor never saw; it would fail for JEPA's asymmetric read modes, not for anything about direct-vs-iterated prediction. ⚠ **State which "beyond H" you mean: Δt = 151 (paper, K=150) or 126 (CAFE hard-codes `horizon_max=125`).** ⚠ **Prerequisite carried from w18: a CLU rollout that beats persistence at n=1 — which does not currently exist** (✅ **R19-1 RESOLVED at w23: the disagreement was the launch frame; under the honest L-1 launch CLU loses at every horizon, 0.7705 vs 0.5669 — the prerequisite is confirmed unmet, not merely contested**). ⚠ And the analyst's own recommendation: *"do not attempt to quote a persistence win until a learned map exists"* — today, with identity `φ` and a handcrafted `ψ`, CLU loses at L-1 in **raw and feature space** (feature space 20–56× worse). Status: `OPEN`.
- **⭐ FD002 (C-MAPSS-2) is the cell worth winning, and its bar is 0.70 — not 0.58.** *What we haven't shown:* a multi-regime win. *Why it matters:* under the **matched-head** protocol **MOMENT (0.70), Moirai (0.66) and TFM-2.5 (0.60) all beat HEPA (0.58)** on C-MAPSS-2 — the earlier *"FD002 is winnable because everyone is near chance"* was read off Table 1 and is **wrong**. FD002 = **6 operating conditions = multi-regime = multi-basin**, exactly the program's own claim; w18 measured CLU **0.6541** vs raw-stats **0.6908** there. *Beating a 341M-param broad-pretrained FM on the multi-regime cell with a ~2M structured primitive is a far stronger claim than beating HEPA on FD001.* ⚠ **The op-condition-vs-fault-mode decomposition that motivates it is POST-HOC — pre-register before quoting** (measured deficits: FD001 −0.0256 · FD003 −0.0261 · FD002 −0.0367 · FD004 −0.0627; a second fault mode costs −0.0005). Status: `OPEN`.
- **Externally comparable C-MAPSS numbers (true-RUL labels) beyond FD001.** *What we haven't shown:* CLU evaluated under corrected labels on FD002/003/004. *Why it matters:* CAFE mislabels **100% of test windows** (mean error +62.5 / +58.9 / +62.7 / +71.7 cycles) because `RUL_FD00x.txt` is never opened — worth **≈ +0.19–0.21 h-AUROC** — so **no CAFE C-MAPSS number is externally comparable to any published result**. The corrected-label ablation exists **only on FD001** (raw_stats **0.9545**, CLU basin **0.9093**, CLU ALL **0.9054**). ✅ *Ranking is label-robust, so the program's relative conclusions survive* — but any external claim needs the corrected protocol. Status: `OPEN` (the forwardable bug report is `.claude/outputs/cmapss-fd002-004-fetch/CAFE_BUG_REPORT.md`; the Head is taking it to the CAFE team).
- **Cross-ref, not duplicated:** the **context-window confound** (HEPA = full engine history vs CAFE `window=30`; per-context instance norm vs train-stat z-score; K=150 vs 125) originates here (`scout-hepa-predictor`, w18) and is already carried under *Engineering debts* as **"Context sweep + HEPA-protocol loader replication (one job, not two)"** — **the largest single candidate for the 0.918/0.81/0.73 spread**, and it may specifically handicap a *dynamical* model. Status: `OPEN` (see that entry).

## Structural-diversity regularizers — the SDR family (wave-17 fold, added 2026-07-23; specified, never built)

> **Context.** `anti-collapse-characterization` (w17) unified the program's measured collapses under **T1** (*a lever collapses iff its order parameter is **wake-invisible***, drivers D1 lr-partition / D2 CD-sleep bias / D3 measure-zero non-formation) and proved **T2** (*anti-collapse = gauge fixing; **zero expressivity price at the optimum***). It then specified a family of regularizers to do the gauge fixing. **The theory is delivered; the family is unbuilt.**

- **⭐ The SDR family R-1 … R-6 (+ `R_specW`) is specified and has never been run in the real trainer.** R-1 mass-spread · R-2 energy anchor *(already exists)* · R-3 Fourier-anchor · R-4 orbit-variance / symmetry-inducer · R-5 band-coverage · R-6 coercivity floor, plus a sorted-Wasserstein spectral pin — each with a derivation, a task-nullity (free vs priced) classification, and a lever→CAFE-task fatality map. *Why it matters:* **T2 says the gauge fixing is free at the optimum**, and the levers it fixes are exactly the ones the program has repeatedly measured as collapsed (N7/CM-5, CM-6/CM-16, N46, N64). ⚠ **The finite-training-time price is real and measured: optimization stiffness, not representability** — the anchor envelope holds the vacuum 5/5 seeds at λ=100 but sits at **35× the wake-MSE floor**. *Where it lands:* V2/V3 + the ICLR long. Status: `OPEN` — **HELD at w17 behind horizon-conditioning (Head), gated on the `dt` fix at w19 (N63), and the gate OPENED at w20**: R-1's relative gradient goes **1.77e-6 → 7.5e-2 (42,000×)** at the corrected `dt` and the λ scan spans `M_max/M_min` **1.35 → 11.4** — ⚠ **but R-1 costs stability (λ=50 drives `dt·ω` to 29.6 via `M_min`) ⇒ any SDR sweep must retune `dt` per λ.**
- **⭐ Assignment — not diversity — is the unsolved half (T3), and nothing in the program addresses it.** *What we haven't shown:* any mechanism that chooses **which channel gets which timescale**. *Why it matters:* **T3 proves that any regularizer built from a permutation-symmetric spectral statistic (variance, entropy, Wasserstein-to-target) can restore *spread* but can never choose *assignment*** — warm-started, the final ordering equals the sign of the **1e-3 init jitter, 10/10**. The family's own candidate patch fails this way. **Binding design rule that follows, and it has never been tested inside the real trainer: structural regularizers ON FROM EPOCH 0, never bolted onto a warm start; prefer the Var-form (gradient ∝ Δ ⇒ the collapsed point becomes an *unstable* fixed point) over a Std-hinge (O(1) subgradient at Δ=0, kicks in an arbitrary direction).** ⚠ This is the same "structure must be supplied, not self-organized" tension the ledger records as **R19-4 (open)** — *do not read T3 as settling it.* Status: `OPEN`.
- **Horizon-conditioning as the *alternative* to SDR (the theorist's arm D, promoted by the w17 measurement).** *What we haven't shown:* an objective in which **degradation phase is wake-VISIBLE**. *Why it matters:* the HCD objective is **degradation-agnostic** — epochs 150→600 is a **null lever** (0.7158 → 0.7147, within seed noise; *"training length" is falsified as a top-3 lever*) — so degradation phase is a **wake-invisible order parameter** and arm D applies directly: *when the order parameter is wake-visible there is no collapse and no regularizer is needed.* ⇒ **horizon-conditioning should be tried BEFORE the SDR family**, which reverses the pre-run plan. *Where it lands:* `clu-horizon-encoder` (**unwritten**; re-gated at w19 on the read-in/`ψ` question, **not** on the withdrawn persistence gate). Status: `OPEN`.

## Positioning & prior-art debts (NEW, wave-19 — reviewer-facing, all unfunded)

- **⛔ The HiPPO challenge, with no answer.** **HiPPO-LegS Prop 3 is *exactly* timescale-equivariant and has NO discretization step `Δt` at all**, with error `O(tL/√N)` and gradient `Θ(1/t)`. CLU's `m` is a *learned* timescale and CLU has an explicit `dt` (⚠ and per **N63** an unresolved `dt`-vs-cycle units bug). ***A reviewer who knows HiPPO will ask why a learned mass spectrum is better than an architecture provably invariant to timescale by construction. We do not currently have an answer.*** *Why it matters:* it is the single sharpest unanswered reviewer question the program owns. Status: `OPEN` (**needs an argument, not an experiment — or an honest concession**).
- **⭐ SRNN's "initial state optimization" — the highest preemption risk we know of.** Chen et al. (ICLR 2020) *"leverages symplectic integration, multiple-step training and **initial state optimization**"* — **the mechanical half of "learn `(q₀,p₀)` as an address"**, though apparently as a *numerical-conditioning device for trajectory fitting* rather than as addressing. ⚠ **The passage could NOT be extracted** (page content insufficient). ***"The engineer or curator must read §Initial State Optimization before we claim learned-`(q₀,p₀)` novelty — this is the single most likely place a reviewer finds us preempted."*** Status: `OPEN` (**blocking on any learned-address novelty claim; needs a tool that can render arXiv:1909.13334 in full**).
- **Ramsauer Theorem 3 (the exact capacity constant) — needed before any capacity sentence is printed.** The qualitative claim (exponential in dimension) is verified from the abstract; **the exponent form `N ≥ √p·c^((d−1)/4)` is SINGLE-SOURCED secondary and must NOT enter a paper** without someone reading the theorem. Status: `OPEN` (try the ICLR 2021 camera-ready or the `hopfield-layers` GitHub companion).
- **The "no Hamiltonian addressable memory exists" negative needs one confirmatory sweep.** Currently a **negative from four targeted searches**, which the scout itself rates **weak evidence** at *"moderate-to-low confidence."* Remedy: a **Semantic Scholar citation-graph walk forward from SRNN and from Kong et al. 2024**. Status: `OPEN` (**gates the novelty claim, not the science**).
- **Two smaller verification debts.** **UnICORNN's Hamiltonian claim and integrator** — unobtained, treat as unverified. **PonderNet's arXiv ID and author list** — from training knowledge, **not verified this session**; verify before citing. Status: `OPEN`.
- **MQAR / associative-recall benchmark specifics.** If the program reports any retrieval diagnostic it **must be the one the field uses** (Zoology / Based line, Arora et al.) — ⚠ *"a memory architecture that does not report them will be assumed to fail them"* (Jelassi et al. made copying/recall **the** discriminating tasks for any fixed-state architecture). Status: `OPEN` (**a prerequisite for the defensible positioning, not an optional extra**).
- **⭐ SC-JEPA (ex-`MTS-JEPA`) — a novelty collision on the multi-regime story, plus the template for our own claim** *(w18 fold, added 2026-07-23)*. **arXiv:2602.04643, SDM 2026 (peer-reviewed, not a preprint), He/Wen/Wang/Ma.** Its **soft codebook = discrete regime codes**, uncomfortably close to CLU's *"multi-basin ⇒ multi-regime"* framing; ⚠ **citing it as "MTS-JEPA" will look stale to a reviewer.** *Why it matters, both ways:* (i) it must be cited and distinguished before any multi-regime novelty claim; (ii) **useful ammunition — SC-JEPA LOSES on C-MAPSS-2 (0.53 vs HEPA 0.57), so discrete regime codes alone do NOT crack the multi-regime cell**; (iii) **HEPA App. H (SC-JEPA vs HEPA, same context length / patch size / matched-capacity head, *"only the SSL objective differs"*) is the cleanest published SSL-objective-only ablation in the field and is the design template for CLU's encoder-swap claim.** ⚠ Corrections that travel with it: **HEPA wins 6 of 7, not 8 of 9**, and **TEP is excluded because the public release has no chemical-process benchmark** (not encoder non-convergence). Status: `OPEN` (**cite-and-distinguish debt; blocking on multi-regime novelty wording, not on the science**).
- **Karuvally's traveling-waves paper (arXiv:2402.10163) argues AGAINST register-like storage** — it stores data **as waves updated by boundary conditions**, explicitly rejecting *"static, register-like locations."* ⚠ **A direct challenge to the coset-register route; worth reading before committing to registers.** Status: `OPEN`.

## Engineering debts surfaced by the w19 audit (small, cheap, unfunded)

- **The `dt = 1.0` end-to-end re-run.** The units verdict is measured *mechanistically* but **no full FD001 arm was run at `dt=1.0`**. *Why it matters:* it plausibly moves `E_reg` out of dominance, **makes R-1 usable, un-freezes the mass spectrum, and makes the wake MSE actually train `V_θ`** — i.e. it may dissolve several negatives at once. ⚠ **Caution: at `dt=1.0` the potential is strong enough to collapse `q*` to a single point under damping — the relax budget must be retuned simultaneously. DO NOT vary `dt` alone.** Status: **`→ SHOWN (wave-20, dt-units-split)`** — `data_dt` split from the integrator `dt`, retuned to **`dt=0.125`** (the task's own caution proved right: naive `dt=1.0` was integrator-unstable and the apparent −0.08 was a pure artifact), 6/6 preregs held, **FD001-NEUTRAL** (0.6540→0.6569 / 0.7158→0.7125, within seed noise; the raw-stats 0.7486 gap unchanged). Two w19 conclusions REVERSED (`H≈V` corr 0.945; spectrum *expands*); R-1's gate **opened** (1.77e-6→7.5e-2). ✅ **R20-1 RESOLVED at w23: the 98.3% is RETIRED as a 40-epoch under-trained fit** — quote **≈77–80% ballistic at legacy `dt`, ≈50% at the shipped `dt=0.125`** (dt-units' 79.7% and 50.6% both confirmed); the carrier was training maturity, not `dt`. See N63's Update block.
- **Train the wake rollout at the γ used at encode time.** Measured mismatch: training rolls **only from window frame 0 at γ=0**, while the dominant inference feature `q*` rolls **from frame L−2 at γ=0.1 (0.5 tuned)** ⇒ **the damped dynamics that generates our embedding is never trained**, and the only tuned lever we have (`relax_budget`, +0.061) operates on an **untrained dynamical regime**. *Cheapest possible fix, no architecture change.* **Worth trying before any `φ`/`ψ` redesign.** Status: `OPEN`.
- **Softplus reparameterization / zero-mean the `log_mass`.** Without bounding the common mode, **any mass-hierarchy mechanism is fighting the `E_reg` runaway** and a log-scale spread stops buying exponential range. Status: **largely DISCHARGED (wave-20, mass-visible-objective):** the softplus trap is a **near-dead issue post-`dt`-fix** (exp reparameterization buys only **1.089×** — stop quoting it as a live blocker), and the real fix landed at the source: **mass-perturbed negatives** (`neg_momentum_scale`, ships OFF) take the contrastive mass-gradient share 0.00%→42.70% and `M_max/M_min` to 4.1051; spectrum functional, τ∝M^0.79. ⚠ **No task metric measured** — turning the knob ON and measuring FD001 is the residual `OPEN` item.
- **The `conftest.py` x64 fix (repo-wide test landmine).** Six test modules enable x64 at module import ⇒ full-suite and standalone runs execute different precisions (**measured flip 0.906→0.594**; bit again at w22 — a real x64 crash in the CLU rollout, since fixed `a7ebf11`). Fixed locally with a fixture; **wants a `conftest.py`-level guard** so no future numerically-marginal test is order-dependent. Status: `OPEN` (N72; small, cheap).
- **Context sweep + HEPA-protocol loader replication (one job, not two).** CAFE's C-MAPSS loader **hardcodes `window=30`**; varying context needs an HEPA-style full-engine cycle-as-patch loader — *task-sized, not a fold-in*, and **both needs want the same loader**. ⚠ **This remains a LIVE CONFOUND on the headline number:** we gave a dynamical primitive a **30-cycle window out of a ~200-cycle degradation** and then measured that it barely beats `q_last`. Status: `OPEN` (**explicitly deferred by the w19 audit as its un-done item 6**).
- **voraus re-verification after the `mass_lr_mult` port.** The port is default-no-op and C-MAPSS default-arm numbers reproduce bit-for-bit, so voraus is *believed* unaffected — **but it was not re-run** and shares `_SharedCLUFit`. ⚠ **Escalated at w20: `dt-units-split` CHANGED SHIPPED DEFAULTS** — this is no longer hygiene; **voraus numbers must not be quoted until re-run** (carried in every wave's OWED list since). Status: `OPEN`.
